tool6. Command (exit 0)
/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import csv,itertools,math
from collections import defaultdict
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
parts=list(csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv')))
recipes={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipes.csv'))}
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}
autos={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
elig=defaultdict(dict)
for e in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')): elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
vm=defaultdict(dict)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/vacuum_manifold_limits.csv')): vm[r['autoclave_id']][r['side']]=int(r['max_vacuum_ports'])
by=defaultdict(list)
for p in parts: by[p['recipe_id']].append(p)

def common_window(grp):
    rel=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in grp)
    latest=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in grp)
    return rel,latest

def quick_packable(grp, aid):
    A=autos[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); H=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    rid=grp[0]['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]; e=elig[rid].get(aid)
    if not e: return False
    if len(grp)>int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']) or len({p['program_id'] for p in grp})>int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']) or sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)>int(rule['max_coupon_panels']): return False
    if sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in grp) > float(rule['max_area_fraction'])*W*H+1e-9: return False
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in grp)>int(A['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in grp)>int(A['thermocouple_ports']): return False
    for p in grp:
        if float(p['thickness_mm'])>float(e['max_thickness_mm'])+1e-9 or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])>int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']): return False
        w=max(float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])) if int(p['rotate_ok']) else float(p['width_cm'])
        h=max(float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])) if int(p['rotate_ok']) else float(p['height_cm'])
        if min(float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])) > H+1e-9: return False
        if min(float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])) > half+1e-9 and max(float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])) > half+1e-9: return False
    return True

for rid,grp in sorted(by.items()):
    rule=rules[rid]
    maxk=int(rule['max_parts_per_batch'])
    total=0; candidates=0; bysize=defaultdict(int); byprog=defaultdict(int)
    for k in range(1,maxk+1):
        for sub in itertools.combinations(grp,k):
            total+=1
            rel,latest=common_window(sub)
            if rel>latest+1e-9: continue
            if len({p['program_id'] for p in sub})>int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']): continue
            if any(quick_packable(sub,aid) for aid in autos):
                candidates+=1; bysize[k]+=1; byprog[len({p['program_id'] for p in sub})]+=1
    print(rid,'total_subsets',total,'feasible',candidates,'bysize',dict(bysize),'byprog',dict(byprog))
PY"

assistant10. Assistant message
I’m extracting the top exact-cover partitions per recipe now, scored for restoration rather than cost: fewer batches first, then wider common windows and more autoclave options. The next step is to feed those into a time-indexed repair MIP for the actual schedule.
tool11. Command (exit 1)
/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import csv,itertools,math
from collections import defaultdict
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
parts=list(csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv')))
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}
autos={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
elig=defaultdict(dict)
for e in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')): elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
vm=defaultdict(dict)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/vacuum_manifold_limits.csv')): vm[r['autoclave_id']][r['side']]=int(r['max_vacuum_ports'])
by=defaultdict(list)
for p in parts: by[p['recipe_id']].append(p)

def common_window(grp):
    rel=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in grp)
    latest=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in grp)
    return rel,latest

def quick_packable(grp, aid):
    A=autos[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); H=float(A['height_cm'])
    rid=grp[0]['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]; e=elig[rid].get(aid)
    if not e: return False
    if len(grp)>int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']) or len({p['program_id'] for p in grp})>int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']) or sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)>int(rule['max_coupon_panels']): return False
    if sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in grp) > float(rule['max_area_fraction'])*W*H+1e-9: return False
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in grp)>int(A['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in grp)>int(A['thermocouple_ports']): return False
    for p in grp:
        if float(p['thickness_mm'])>float(e['max_thickness_mm'])+1e-9 or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])>int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']): return False
    return True

for rid,grp in sorted(by.items()):
    cands=[]
    maxk=int(rules[rid]['max_parts_per_batch'])
    for k in range(1,maxk+1):
        for sub in itertools.combinations(grp,k):
            rel,latest=common_window(sub)
            if rel>latest+1e-9: continue
            progs=len({p['program_id'] for p in sub})
            if progs>int(rules[rid]['max_programs_per_batch']): continue
            autos_ok=[aid for aid in autos if quick_packable(sub,aid)]
            if not autos_ok: continue
            width=latest-rel
            size=len(sub)
            score=1000000 - 1000*size - 5*len(autos_ok) - width/100.0  # lower better when minimizing cover obj later? we'll maximize neg
            cands.append({'parts':[p['part_id'] for p in sub],'autos':autos_ok,'width':width,'size':size,'progs':progs,'score':score})
    print('\\nRECIPE',rid,'cands',len(cands))
    m=Model(); m.setParam('display/verblevel',0)
    y=[m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'y{i}') for i in range(len(cands))]
    for p in grp:
        m.addCons(quicksum(y[i] for i,c in enumerate(cands) if p['part_id'] in c['parts']) == 1)
    # minimize num batches, then maximize width/autos/size
    m.setObjective(quicksum(1000000*y[i] - 100*cands[i]['width']*y[i] - 1000*len(cands[i]['autos'])*y[i] - cands[i]['size']*y[i] for i in range(len(cands))), 'minimize')
    seen=[]
    for k in range(8):
        m.optimize()
        if m.getNSols()==0: break
        sol=[i for i in range(len(cands)) if m.getVal(y[i])>0.5]
        print('sol',k,'nbatches',len(sol))
        for i in sol:
            print(' ',sorted(cands[i]['parts']), 'autos',cands[i]['autos'],'width',cands[i]['width'])
        m.addCons(quicksum(y[i] for i in sol) <= len(sol)-1)
PY"

import csv,itertools,math
from collections import defaultdict
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
parts=list(csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv')))
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}
autos={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
elig=defaultdict(dict)
for e in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')): elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
by=defaultdict(list)
for p in parts: by[p['recipe_id']].append(p)

def common_window(grp):
    rel=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in grp)
    latest=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in grp)
    return rel,latest

def quick_packable(grp, aid):
    A=autos[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); H=float(A['height_cm'])
    rid=grp[0]['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]; e=elig[rid].get(aid)
    if not e: return False
    if len(grp)>int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']) or len({p['program_id'] for p in grp})>int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']) or sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)>int(rule['max_coupon_panels']): return False
    if sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in grp) > float(rule['max_area_fraction'])*W*H+1e-9: return False
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in grp)>int(A['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in grp)>int(A['thermocouple_ports']): return False
    for p in grp:
        if float(p['thickness_mm'])>float(e['max_thickness_mm'])+1e-9 or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])>int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']): return False
    return True

for rid,grp in sorted(by.items()):
    cands=[]
    maxk=int(rules[rid]['max_parts_per_batch'])
    for k in range(1,maxk+1):
        for sub in itertools.combinations(grp,k):
            rel,latest=common_window(sub)
            if rel>latest+1e-9: continue
            progs=len({p['program_id'] for p in sub})
            if progs>int(rules[rid]['max_programs_per_batch']): continue

            if not autos_ok: continue
            width=latest-rel
            cands.append({'parts':[p['part_id'] for p in sub],'autos':autos_ok,'width':width,'size':len(sub),'progs':progs})
    print('\\nRECIPE',rid,'cands',len(cands))
    m=Model(); m.setParam('display/verblevel',0)
    y=[m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'y{i}') for i in range(len(cands))]
    for p in grp:
        m.addCons(quicksum(y[i] for i,c in enumerate(cands) if p['part_id'] in c['parts']) == 1)
    m.setObjective(quicksum(1000000*y[i] - 100*cands[i]['width']*y[i] - 1000*len(cands[i]['autos'])*y[i] - cands[i]['size']*y[i] for i in range(len(cands))), 'minimize')
    for k in range(5):
        m.optimize()
        if m.getNSols()==0: break
        sol=[i for i in range(len(cands)) if m.getVal(y[i])>0.5]
        print('sol',k,'nbatches',len(sol))
        for i in sol:
            print(' ',sorted(cands[i]['parts']), 'autos',cands[i]['autos'],'width',cands[i]['width'])
        m.freeTransform()
        m.addCons(quicksum(y[i] for i in sol) <= len(sol)-1)

assistant15. Assistant message
I’m now testing the top recipe partitions with an exact event-sweep validator and a DFS scheduler on a coarse start grid. If one combination passes, I’ll use that as the incumbent and then repair/refine around it rather than continuing blind partition guesses.
assistant22. Assistant message
The combinational DFS is running over the strongest partition families. If it doesn’t return quickly, I’ll stop it and move the same partition menu into a Phase-I SCIP model so the search can use explicit violation slacks instead of pure backtracking.

def cap_at(intervals,t):
    for s,e,c in intervals:
        if s<=t<e: return c
    return None

def interval_overlap(a,b,c,d):
    return max(a,c) < min(b,d) - 1e-9

def make_batches(choice):
    batches=[]; idx=1
    for rid in ['R_BMI_HIGH','R_FAST_EPOXY','R_RADOME_LOW','R_STD_EPOXY','R_TOUGHENED']:
        for grp in partitions[rid][choice[rid]]:
            batch={'id':f'B{idx:02d}','recipe_id':rid,'parts':grp}
            ps=[parts[p] for p in grp]
            batch['programs']=sorted({p['program_id'] for p in ps})
            batch['release']=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in ps)
            batch['latest']=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in ps)
            batch['fixture_counts']=Counter(p['fixture_type'] for p in ps)
            batch['count']=len(ps); batch['ndt_count']=sum(int(p['ndt_required']) for p in ps); batch['coupon_count']=sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in ps)
            batch['autos']=[]; batch['dur']=dict(); batch['load']=dict(); batch['cure']=dict(); batch['cool']=dict(); batch['insp']=dict()
            for aid,a in autoclaves.items():
                e=elig[rid].get(aid)
                if not e: continue
                if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in ps)>int(a['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in ps)>int(a['thermocouple_ports']): continue
                if any(float(p['thickness_mm'])>float(e['max_thickness_mm'])+1e-9 or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])>int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']) for p in ps): continue
                batch['autos'].append(aid)
                rec=recipes[rid]; rule=rules[rid]
                load=float(rec['load_fixed_min'])+float(rec['load_per_part_min'])*batch['count']
                cure=float(rec['cure_min'])*float(e['process_multiplier'])
                dur=load+cure+float(rec['unload_min'])
                cool=float(rule['cooling_fixed_min'])+float(rule['cooling_per_part_min'])*batch['count']
                insp=(float(rule['ndt_minutes_fixed'])+float(rule['ndt_minutes_per_part'])*batch['ndt_count']+float(config['coupon_panel_inspection_minutes'])*batch['coupon_count']) if batch['ndt_count']>0 else 0.0
                batch['load'][aid]=load; batch['cure'][aid]=cure; batch['dur'][aid]=dur; batch['cool'][aid]=cool; batch['insp'][aid]=insp
            batches.append(batch); idx+=1
    return batches

def partial_feasible(sol,batch_map):
    by_auto=defaultdict(list); fix=defaultdict(list); load_ev=defaultdict(list); cure_ev=[]; cool_ev=[]; insp_ev=[]
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]; aid=ent['autoclave_id']; s=ent['start']; e=s+b['dur'][aid]
        if aid not in b['autos'] or s < b['release']-1e-9 or s > b['latest']+1e-9 or e > H+1e-9: return False
        for mw in maint[aid]:
            if interval_overlap(s,e,*mw): return False
        by_auto[aid].append((s,e,b,aid))
        for ft,qty in b['fixture_counts'].items(): fix[ft].append((s,e,qty))
        load_ev[autoclaves[aid]['operator_skill']].append((s,s+b['load'][aid],1))
        cure_ev.append((s+b['load'][aid],s+b['load'][aid]+b['cure'][aid],1))
        cool_ev.append((e,e+b['cool'][aid],1))
        if b['insp'][aid]>0: insp_ev.append((e+b['cool'][aid],e+b['cool'][aid]+b['insp'][aid],1))
    for aid,lst in by_auto.items():
        lst.sort()
        for i in range(1,len(lst)):
            ps,pe,pb,_=lst[i-1]; s,e,b,_=lst[i]
            gap=setup[(recipes[pb['recipe_id']]['resin_family'],recipes[b['recipe_id']]['resin_family'])]
            if s < pe + gap - 1e-9: return False
    def check(events,caps):
        pts=sorted({0.0,H} | {x for s,e,_ in events for x in (s,e)} | {x for s,e,_ in caps for x in (s,e)})
        for l,r in zip(pts,pts[1:]):
            if r-l<=1e-9: continue
            m=(l+r)/2
            use=sum(v for s,e,v in events if s<=m<e)
            cap=cap_at(caps,m)
            if cap is not None and use>cap+1e-9: return False
        return True
    if not check(cure_ev,energy): return False
    if not check(cool_ev,cooling): return False
    if not check(insp_ev,inspect): return False
    for sk,ev in load_ev.items():
        if not check(ev,ops[sk]): return False
    for ft,ev in fix.items():
        if not check(ev,[(0.0,H,fixtures[ft])]): return False
    return True

def full_feasible(sol,batch_map):
    if not partial_feasible(sol,batch_map): return False
    prog=defaultdict(int)
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]; aid=ent['autoclave_id']; end=ent['start']+b['dur'][aid]
        for prog_id in b['programs']:
            due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
            if end<=due+1e-9:
                prog[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in b['parts'] if parts[p]['program_id']==prog_id)
    for rr in commit:
        total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id'])
        req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total-1e-9)
        if prog[rr['program_id']]<req: return False
    return True

def search_batches(batches, step=15.0, timelimit=2.0):
    t0=time.time(); batch_map={b['id']:b for b in batches}; visited=0
    for b in batches:
        if not b['autos']: return None
    def candidate_times(b, sol):
        out=[]
        for aid in b['autos']:
            times=[]
            lb=int(math.ceil(b['release']))
            ub=int(math.floor(b['latest']))
            vals=set([float(lb),float(ub)])
            t=lb - (lb % int(step)) if step>=1 else lb
            if t<lb: t+=int(step)
            while t<=ub:
                vals.add(float(t)); t+=int(step)
            for ent in sol:
                bb=batch_map[ent['id']]; aa=ent['autoclave_id']
                vals.add(ent['start']+bb['dur'][aa])
                vals.add(ent['start']+bb['dur'][aa]+setup[(recipes[bb['recipe_id']]['resin_family'],recipes[b['recipe_id']]['resin_family'])])
            cand=[]
            for tt in sorted(v for v in vals if lb-1e-9<=v<=ub+1e-9):
                ent={'id':b['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':tt}
                if partial_feasible(sol+[ent],batch_map):
                    cand.append((tt+b['dur'][aid], tt, aid))
            out.extend(cand[:12])
        out=sorted(set(out))
        return [(aid,t) for _,t,aid in out[:20]]
    def optimistic(sol, rem):
        prog=defaultdict(int); extra=defaultdict(int)
        for ent in sol:
            b=batch_map[ent['id']]; aid=ent['autoclave_id']; end=ent['start']+b['dur'][aid]
            for prog_id in b['programs']:
                due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
                if end<=due+1e-9:
                    prog[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in b['parts'] if parts[p]['program_id']==prog_id)
        for b in rem:
            minend=min(b['release']+b['dur'][aid] for aid in b['autos'])
            for prog_id in b['programs']:
                due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
                if minend<=due+1e-9:
                    extra[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in b['parts'] if parts[p]['program_id']==prog_id)
        for rr in commit:
            total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id']); req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total-1e-9)
            if prog[rr['program_id']]+extra[rr['program_id']]<req: return False
        return True
    def dfs(sol, rem):
        nonlocal visited
        visited+=1
        if time.time()-t0>timelimit: return None
        if not rem:
            return sol if full_feasible(sol,batch_map) else None
        if not optimistic(sol, rem): return None
        domains=[]
        for b in rem:
            cand=candidate_times(b, sol)
            if not cand: return None
            domains.append((len(cand), len(b['autos']), b['latest']-b['release'], b['release'], b, cand))
        domains.sort(key=lambda x:(x[0],x[1],x[2],x[3]))
        _,_,_,_,b,cand=domains[0]
        rest=[r for r in rem if r['id']"'!=b['"'id']]
        for aid,t in cand:
            res=dfs(sol+[{'id':b['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':t}], rest)
            if res is not None: return res
        return None
    res=dfs([], batches)
    return res, visited

order=['R_BMI_HIGH','R_FAST_EPOXY','R_RADOME_LOW','R_STD_EPOXY','R_TOUGHENED']
count=0
for choice_vals in itertools.product(range(3), repeat=5):
    choice=dict(zip(order, choice_vals))
    batches=make_batches(choice)
    sol,visited=search_batches(batches, step=15.0, timelimit=1.5)
    count+=1
    print('combo',count,choice,'visited',visited,'found',sol is not None)
    if sol is not None:
        print('SOLUTION',choice)
        for ent in sorted(sol,key=lambda x:x['start']): print(ent)
        break
    if count>=80: break

import csv, json
from collections import defaultdict
parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autoclaves=['AC1','AC2','AC3']

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import csv, json, math
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autoclaves={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
recipes={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipes.csv'))}
elig=defaultdict(dict)
for e in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')): elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}
fixtures={r['fixture_type']:int(r['capacity']) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/fixtures.csv'))}
setup={(r['from_resin_family'],r['to_resin_family']):float(r['setup_min']) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/setup_times.csv'))}
maint=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/maintenance_windows.csv')): maint[r['autoclave_id']].append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min'])))
ops=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/operator_calendar.csv')): ops[r['skill']].append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])))
energy=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['max_active_autoclaves'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/energy_tariffs.csv'))]
cooling=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/cooling_lane_capacity.csv'))]
inspect=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/post_cure_inspection_capacity.csv'))]
commit=[r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/program_commitments.csv'))]
config=json.load(open('/app/data/config.json'))
H=float(config['planning_horizon_min'])

def cap_at(intervals,t):
    for s,e,c in intervals:
        if s<=t<e: return c
    return None

def overlap(a,b,c,d): return max(a,c)<min(b,d)-1e-9
batches={
'T1':(['P002','P004','P019'],'AC1',637.0),
'BMI2':(['P001','P003','P016','P018','P031','P046'],'AC2',1435.0),
'RAD1':(['P010','P012','P027'],'AC3',1260.0),
'BMI3':(['P014','P029','P033','P048','P054','P055'],'AC1',1783.0),
'RAD2':(['P025','P040','P042','P056'],'AC3',1891.0),
'STD2':(['P005','P007','P009','P020','P022','P037','P039','P053'],'AC2',2325.0),
'T2':(['P006','P017','P021','P032','P036','P051'],'AC3',2399.0),
'FAST2':(['P011','P013','P015','P028','P030','P045'],'AC1',2533.0),
'FAST1':(['P026','P041','P043','P060','P061'],'AC3',3047.0),
'T3':(['P034','P047','P049','P058','P059','P068'],'AC2',3420.0),
'BMI1':(['P044','P064','P065'],'AC1',3460.0),
'RAD3':(['P008','P023','P038','P057','P066','P067'],'AC3',3526.0),
'STD1':(['P024','P035','P050','P052','P062','P063'],'AC3',4062.0),
}
by_auto=defaultdict(list); fix=defaultdict(list); load_ev=defaultdict(list); cure_ev=[]; cool_ev=[]; insp_ev=[]; prog=defaultdict(int)
for name,(pids,aid,s) in batches.items():
    ps=[parts[p] for p in pids]; rid=ps[0]['recipe_id']; rec=recipes[rid]; e=elig[rid][aid]; rule=rules[rid]
    rel=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in ps); latest=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in ps)
    load=float(rec['load_fixed_min'])+float(rec['load_per_part_min'])*len(ps)
    cure=float(rec['cure_min'])*float(e['process_multiplier'])
    dur=load+cure+float(rec['unload_min'])
    end=s+dur
    cool=float(rule['cooling_fixed_min'])+float(rule['cooling_per_part_min'])*len(ps)
    insp=(float(rule['ndt_minutes_fixed'])+float(rule['ndt_minutes_per_part'])*sum(int(p['ndt_required']) for p in ps)+float(config['coupon_panel_inspection_minutes'])*sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in ps)) if sum(int(p['ndt_required']) for p in ps)>0 else 0.0
    print(name,rid,aid,'win',rel,latest,'end',end)
    if s<rel-1e-9 or s>latest+1e-9 or end>H+1e-9: print('WINDOW FAIL',name)
    for mw in maint[aid]:
        if overlap(s,end,*mw): print('MAINT FAIL',name,mw)
    by_auto[aid].append((s,end,rid,name))
    for ft,q in Counter(p['fixture_type'] for p in ps).items(): fix[ft].append((s,end,q,name))
    load_ev[autoclaves[aid]['operator_skill']].append((s,s+load,1,name)); cure_ev.append((s+load,s+load+cure,1,name)); cool_ev.append((end,end+cool,1,name));
    if insp>0: insp_ev.append((end+cool,end+cool+insp,1,name))
    for prog_id in {p['program_id'] for p in ps}:
        due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
        if end<=due+1e-9: prog[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in ps if p['program_id']==prog_id)
for aid,lst in by_auto.items():
    lst=sorted(lst)
    print('AUTO',aid,lst)
    for i in range(1,len(lst)):
        ps,pe,pr,pn=lst[i-1]; s,e,r,n=lst[i]
        gap=setup[(recipes[pr]['resin_family'],recipes[r]['resin_family'])]
        if s < pe+gap-1e-9: print('SETUP FAIL',aid,pn,n,pe,gap,s)

def check(events,caps,name):
    pts=sorted({0.0,H}|{x for s,e,_,_ in events for x in (s,e)}|{x for s,e,_ in caps for x in (s,e)})
    bad=[]
    for l,r in zip(pts,pts[1:]):
        if r-l<=1e-9: continue
        m=(l+r)/2
        use=[nm for s,e,v,nm in events if s<=m<e]
        cap=cap_at(caps,m)
        if cap is not None and len(use)>cap:
            bad.append((l,r,len(use),cap,use))
    print(name,'badcount',len(bad))
    for row in bad[:10]: print(' ',row)
check(cure_ev,energy,'energy')
check(cool_ev,cooling,'cool')
check(insp_ev,inspect,'insp')
for sk,ev in load_ev.items(): check(ev,ops[sk],f'load {sk}')
for ft,ev in fix.items(): check(ev,[(0.0,H,fixtures[ft])],f'fix {ft}')
print('commit',dict(prog))
for rr in commit:
    total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id']); req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total-1e-9)
    print(rr['program_id'],prog[rr['program_id']],req)

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import csv, json, math
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autoclaves={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
recipes={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipes.csv'))}
elig=defaultdict(dict)
for e in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')): elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}
fixtures={r['fixture_type']:int(r['capacity']) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/fixtures.csv'))}
setup={(r['from_resin_family'],r['to_resin_family']):float(r['setup_min']) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/setup_times.csv'))}
maint=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/maintenance_windows.csv')): maint[r['autoclave_id']].append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min'])))
ops=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/operator_calendar.csv')): ops[r['skill']].append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])))
energy=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['max_active_autoclaves'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/energy_tariffs.csv'))]
cooling=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/cooling_lane_capacity.csv'))]
inspect=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/post_cure_inspection_capacity.csv'))]
commit=[r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/program_commitments.csv'))]
config=json.load(open('/app/data/config.json'))
H=float(config['planning_horizon_min'])

def cap_at(intervals,t):
    for s,e,c in intervals:
        if s<=t<e: return c
    return None

def overlap(a,b,c,d): return max(a,c)<min(b,d)-1e-9
batches={
'T1':(['P002','P004','P019'],'AC1',637.0),
'BMI2':(['P001','P003','P016','P018','P031','P046'],'AC2',1435.0),
'RAD1':(['P010','P012','P027'],'AC3',1260.0),
'BMI3':(['P014','P029','P033','P048','P054','P055'],'AC1',1783.0),
'T2':(['P006','P017','P021','P032','P036','P051'],'AC3',2033.0),
'STD2':(['P005','P007','P009','P020','P022','P037','P039','P053'],'AC2',2325.0),
'FAST2':(['P011','P013','P015','P028','P030','P045'],'AC1',2533.1),
'FAST1':(['P026','P041','P043','P060','P061'],'AC3',2790.0),
'STD1':(['P024','P035','P050','P052','P062','P063'],'AC3',3211.0),
'T3':(['P034','P047','P049','P058','P059','P068'],'AC2',3699.0),
'RAD3':(['P008','P023','P038','P057','P066','P067'],'AC3',3817.0),
'BMI1':(['P044','P064','P065'],'AC1',4185.0),
'RAD2':(['P025','P040','P042','P056'],'AC3',4288.0),
}
by_auto=defaultdict(list); fix=defaultdict(list); load_ev=defaultdict(list); cure_ev=[]; cool_ev=[]; insp_ev=[]; prog=defaultdict(int)
for name,(pids,aid,s) in batches.items():
    ps=[parts[p] for p in pids]; rid=ps[0]['recipe_id']; rec=recipes[rid]; e=elig[rid][aid]; rule=rules[rid]
    rel=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in ps); latest=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in ps)
    load=float(rec['load_fixed_min'])+float(rec['load_per_part_min'])*len(ps)
    cure=float(rec['cure_min'])*float(e['process_multiplier'])
    dur=load+cure+float(rec['unload_min'])
    end=s+dur
    cool=float(rule['cooling_fixed_min'])+float(rule['cooling_per_part_min'])*len(ps)
    insp=(float(rule['ndt_minutes_fixed'])+float(rule['ndt_minutes_per_part'])*sum(int(p['ndt_required']) for p in ps)+float(config['coupon_panel_inspection_minutes'])*sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in ps)) if sum(int(p['ndt_required']) for p in ps)>0 else 0.0
    print(name,rid,aid,'win',rel,latest,'end',end)
    if s<rel-1e-9 or s>latest+1e-9 or end>H+1e-9: print('WINDOW FAIL',name)
    for mw in maint[aid]:
        if overlap(s,end,*mw): print('MAINT FAIL',name,mw)
    by_auto[aid].append((s,end,rid,name))
    for ft,q in Counter(p['fixture_type'] for p in ps).items(): fix[ft].append((s,end,q,name))
    load_ev[autoclaves[aid]['operator_skill']].append((s,s+load,1,name)); cure_ev.append((s+load,s+load+cure,1,name)); cool_ev.append((end,end+cool,1,name));
    if insp>0: insp_ev.append((end+cool,end+cool+insp,1,name))
    for prog_id in {p['program_id'] for p in ps}:
        due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
        if end<=due+1e-9: prog[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in ps if p['program_id']==prog_id)
for aid,lst in by_auto.items():
    lst=sorted(lst)
    print('AUTO',aid,lst)
    for i in range(1,len(lst)):
        ps,pe,pr,pn=lst[i-1]; s,e,r,n=lst[i]
        gap=setup[(recipes[pr]['resin_family'],recipes[r]['resin_family'])]
        if s < pe+gap-1e-9: print('SETUP FAIL',aid,pn,n,pe,gap,s)

def check(events,caps,name):
    pts=sorted({0.0,H}|{x for s,e,_,_ in events for x in (s,e)}|{x for s,e,_ in caps for x in (s,e)})
    bad=[]
    for l,r in zip(pts,pts[1:]):
        if r-l<=1e-9: continue
        m=(l+r)/2
        use=[nm for s,e,v,nm in events if s<=m<e]
        cap=cap_at(caps,m)
        if cap is not None and len(use)>cap:
            bad.append((l,r,len(use),cap,use))
    print(name,'badcount',len(bad))
    for row in bad[:10]: print(' ',row)
check(cure_ev,energy,'energy')
check(cool_ev,cooling,'cool')
check(insp_ev,inspect,'insp')
for sk,ev in load_ev.items(): check(ev,ops[sk],f'load {sk}')
for ft,ev in fix.items(): check(ev,[(0.0,H,fixtures[ft])],f'fix {ft}')
print('commit',dict(prog))
for rr in commit:
    total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id']); req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total-1e-9)
    print(rr['program_id'],prog[rr['program_id']],req)

def overlap(a,b,c,d): return max(a,c)<min(b,d)-1e-9

def check(events,caps):
    pts=sorted({0.0,H}|{x for s,e,_,_ in events for x in (s,e)}|{x for s,e,_ in caps for x in (s,e)})
    for l,r in zip(pts,pts[1:]):
        if r-l<=1e-9: continue
        m=(l+r)/2
        use=sum(v for s,e,v,_ in events if s<=m<e)
        cap=cap_at(caps,m)
        assert cap is None or use<=cap+1e-9, (l,r,use,cap,[nm for s,e,v,nm in events if s<=m<e])

    assert prog[rr['program_id']]>=req,(rr,prog[rr['program_id']],req)

import csv, json
from collections import defaultdict
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autos={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}

selected=[
('B01',['P002','P004','P019'],'AC1'),
('B02',['P001','P003','P016','P018','P031','P046'],'AC2'),
('B03',['P010','P012','P027'],'AC3'),
('B04',['P014','P029','P033','P048','P054','P055'],'AC1'),
('B05',['P006','P017','P021','P032','P036','P051'],'AC3'),
('B06',['P005','P007','P009','P020','P022','P037','P039','P053'],'AC2'),
('B07',['P011','P013','P015','P028','P030','P045'],'AC1'),
('B08',['P026','P041','P043','P060','P061'],'AC3'),
('B09',['P024','P035','P050','P052','P062','P063'],'AC3'),
('B10',['P034','P047','P049','P058','P059','P068'],'AC2'),
('B11',['P008','P023','P038','P057','P066','P067'],'AC3'),
('B12',['P044','P064','P065'],'AC1'),
('B13',['P025','P040','P042','P056'],'AC3'),
]

def solve_layout(part_ids, aid):
    grp=[parts[p] for p in part_ids]
    rid=grp[0]['recipe_id']
    A=autos[aid]
    W=float(A['width_cm']); H=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    m=Model(); m.setParam('display/verblevel',0); m.setParam('limits/time',15)
    x={}; y={}; r={}; l={}; rr={}; w={}; h={}
    for p in grp:
        pid=p['part_id']
        x[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0, ub=W, name=f'x[{pid}]')
        y[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0, ub=H, name=f'y[{pid}]')
        r[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', lb=0, ub=1 if int(p['rotate_ok']) else 0, name=f'r[{pid}]')
        l[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'l[{pid}]')
        rr[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'rr[{pid}]')
        m.addCons(l[pid]+rr[pid]==1)
        w0=float(p['width_cm']); h0=float(p['height_cm'])
        w[pid]=w0+(h0-w0)*r[pid]
        h[pid]=h0+(w0-h0)*r[pid]
        m.addCons(x[pid]+w[pid] <= W)
        m.addCons(y[pid]+h[pid] <= H)
        m.addCons(x[pid]+w[pid] <= half + W*(1-l[pid]))
        m.addCons(x[pid] >= half - W*(1-rr[pid]))
        pref=p['preferred_zone']
        if pref=='center':
            m.addCons(x[pid] >= 1e-3)
            m.addCons(y[pid] >= 1e-3)
            m.addCons(x[pid]+w[pid] <= W-1e-3)
            m.addCons(y[pid]+h[pid] <= H-1e-3)
        elif pref=='edge':
            eL=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eR=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eB=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eT=m.addVar(vtype='B')
            m.addCons(eL+eR+eB+eT >= 1)
            m.addCons(x[pid] <= W*(1-eL))
            m.addCons(x[pid]+w[pid] >= W - W*(1-eR))
            m.addCons(y[pid] <= H*(1-eB))
            m.addCons(y[pid]+h[pid] >= H - H*(1-eT))
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*l[p['part_id']] for p in grp) <= vm[aid]['left'])
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*rr[p['part_id']] for p in grp) <= vm[aid]['right'])
    ids=[p['part_id'] for p in grp]
    for i in range(len(ids)):
        for j in range(i+1,len(ids)):
            a,b=ids[i],ids[j]
            L=m.addVar(vtype='B'); R=m.addVar(vtype='B'); B=m.addVar(vtype='B'); T=m.addVar(vtype='B')
            m.addCons(L+R+B+T >= 1)
            m.addCons(x[a]+w[a] <= x[b] + W*(1-L))
            m.addCons(x[b]+w[b] <= x[a] + W*(1-R))
            m.addCons(y[a]+h[a] <= y[b] + H*(1-B))
            m.addCons(y[b]+h[b] <= y[a] + H*(1-T))
    m.setObjective(quicksum(x[i]+y[i] for i in ids), 'minimize')
    m.optimize()
    return m.getStatus(), m.getNSols()

for bid,pids,aid in selected:
    status,nsol=solve_layout(pids,aid)
    print(bid,aid,status,nsol)

('B01',['P002','P004','P019'],'AC1'),
('B02',['P001','P003','P016','P018','P031','P046'],'AC2'),

('B13',['P025','P040','P042','P056'],'AC3'),

def feasible_pref(global_x,y,w,h,W,H,pref):
    if pref=='center':
        return global_x > 1e-6 and y > 1e-6 and global_x + w < W - 1e-6 and y + h < H - 1e-6
    if pref=='edge':
        return global_x <= 1e-6 or y <= 1e-6 or global_x + w >= W - 1e-6 or y + h >= H - 1e-6
    return True

def overlaps(x,y,w,h,placed):
    for _,px,py,pw,ph,_ in placed:
        if max(x,px) < min(x+w,px+pw) - 1e-9 and max(y,py) < min(y+h,py+ph) - 1e-9:
            return True
    return False

def pack_side(items, side, Wside, Wfull, H):
    items=sorted(items, key=lambda it:(it['pref']=='edge', it['pref']"'!='"'center', -it['h'], -it['w'], it['id']), reverse=True)
    placed=[]
    def rec(k):
        if k==len(items):
            return True
        it=items[k]
        xs={0.0, max(0.0, Wside-it['w'])}
        ys={0.0, max(0.0, H-it['h'])}
        for _,px,py,pw,ph,_ in placed:
            xs.add(px+pw)
            ys.add(py+ph)
        for y in sorted(ys):
            for x in sorted(xs):
                gx = x if side=='left' else Wfull/2 + x
                if x + it['w'] > Wside + 1e-9 or y + it['h'] > H + 1e-9:
                    continue
                if overlaps(x,y,it['w'],it['h'],placed):
                    continue
                if not feasible_pref(gx,y,it['w'],it['h'],Wfull,H,it['pref']):
                    continue
                placed.append((it['id'],x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['rotated']))
                if rec(k+1):
                    return True
                placed.pop()
        return False
    if rec(0):
        return {pid:(x,y,w,h,rot) for pid,x,y,w,h,rot in placed}
    return None

def solve_batch(pids, aid):
    A=autos[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); H=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    grp=[parts[p] for p in pids]
    rot_opts=[[False,True] if int(p['rotate_ok']) else [False] for p in grp]
    for rots in itertools.product(*rot_opts):
        items=[]
        bad=False
        for p,rot in zip(grp,rots):
            w=float(p['height_cm']) if rot else float(p['width_cm'])
            h=float(p['width_cm']) if rot else float(p['height_cm'])
            if w > half + 1e-9 or h > H + 1e-9:
                bad=True
                break
            items.append({'id':p['part_id'],'w':w,'h':h,'pref':p['preferred_zone'],'vac':int(p['vacuum_ports']),'rotated':rot})
        if bad:
            continue
        for sides in itertools.product(['left','right'], repeat=len(items)):
            lv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left')
            rv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right')
            if lv > vm[aid]['left'] or rv > vm[aid]['right']:
                continue
            left_items=[it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left']
            right_items=[it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right']
            left=pack_side(left_items,'left',half,W,H)
            if left is None:
                continue
            right=pack_side(right_items,'right',half,W,H)
            if right is None:
                continue
            out=[]
            for it,s in zip(items,sides):
                x,y,w,h,rot = (left if s=='left' else right)[it['id']]
                gx = x if s=='left' else half + x
                out.append({'part_id':it['id'],'x_cm':round(gx,8),'y_cm':round(y,8),'rotated':rot})
            return out
    return None

all_ok=True
for bid,pids,aid in selected:
    lay=solve_batch(pids,aid)
    print(bid,aid,'OK' if lay else 'FAIL')
    if lay:
        print(json.dumps(lay))
    else:
        all_ok=False
print('ALL_OK',all_ok)

parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autos={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
vm=defaultdict(dict)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/vacuum_manifold_limits.csv')): vm[r['autoclave_id']][r['side']]=int(r['max_vacuum_ports'])
pids=['P024','P035','P050','P052','P062','P063']

def feasible_pref(global_x,y,w,h,W,H,pref):
    if pref=='center':
        return global_x > 1e-6 and y > 1e-6 and global_x + w < W - 1e-6 and y + h < H - 1e-6
    if pref=='edge':
        return global_x <= 1e-6 or y <= 1e-6 or global_x + w >= W - 1e-6 or y + h >= H - 1e-6
    return True

def overlaps(x,y,w,h,placed):
    for _,px,py,pw,ph,_ in placed:
        if max(x,px) < min(x+w,px+pw) - 1e-9 and max(y,py) < min(y+h,py+ph) - 1e-9:
            return True
    return False

def pack_side(items, side, Wside, Wfull, H):
    items=sorted(items, key=lambda it:(it['pref']=='edge', it['pref']"'!='"'center', -it['h'], -it['w'], it['id']), reverse=True)
    placed=[]
    def rec(k):
        if k==len(items): return True
        it=items[k]
        xs={0.0, max(0.0, Wside-it['w'])}
        ys={0.0, max(0.0, H-it['h'])}
        for _,px,py,pw,ph,_ in placed:
            xs.add(px+pw); ys.add(py+ph)
        for y in sorted(ys):
            for x in sorted(xs):
                gx=x if side=='left' else Wfull/2+x
                if x+it['w']>Wside+1e-9 or y+it['h']>H+1e-9: continue
                if overlaps(x,y,it['w'],it['h'],placed): continue
                if not feasible_pref(gx,y,it['w'],it['h'],Wfull,H,it['pref']): continue
                placed.append((it['id'],x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['rotated']))
                if rec(k+1): return True
                placed.pop()
        return False
    if rec(0): return {pid:(x,y,w,h,rot) for pid,x,y,w,h,rot in placed}
    return None

def solve_batch(pids, aid):
    A=autos[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); H=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    grp=[parts[p] for p in pids]
    rot_opts=[[False,True] if int(p['rotate_ok']) else [False] for p in grp]
    for rots in itertools.product(*rot_opts):
        items=[]; bad=False
        for p,rot in zip(grp,rots):
            w=float(p['height_cm']) if rot else float(p['width_cm'])
            h=float(p['width_cm']) if rot else float(p['height_cm'])
            if w>half+1e-9 or h>H+1e-9:
                bad=True; break
            items.append({'id':p['part_id'],'w':w,'h':h,'pref':p['preferred_zone'],'vac':int(p['vacuum_ports']),'rotated':rot})
        if bad: continue
        for sides in itertools.product(['left','right'], repeat=len(items)):
            lv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left')
            rv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right')
            if lv>vm[aid]['left'] or rv>vm[aid]['right']: continue
            left=pack_side([it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left'],'left',half,W,H)
            if left is None: continue
            right=pack_side([it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right'],'right',half,W,H)
            if right is None: continue
            out=[]
            for it,s in zip(items,sides):
                x,y,w,h,rot=(left if s=='left' else right)[it['id']]
                gx=x if s=='left' else half+x
                out.append({'part_id':it['id'],'x_cm':gx,'y_cm':y,'rotated':rot})
            return out
    return None
for aid in ['AC1','AC2','AC3']:
    lay=solve_batch(pids,aid)
    print(aid, 'OK' if lay else 'FAIL', json.dumps(lay) if lay else '')
PY"

---
name: optimization-modeling-core
description: Use for natural-language-to-optimization tasks that require translating a business problem into a complete mathematical model before writing solver code.
metadata:
  short-description: Turn business text into a full optimization model
---

# Optimization Modeling Core

## Use when

- The user gives a word problem, business process, paper scenario, or benchmark prompt and expects a formal optimization model.
- Solver code exists but the issue appears to be a missing or incorrect formulation.
- The task asks for a complete model, not only a heuristic, simulation, or data summary.

## Do not use when

- The task is purely about software plumbing, visualization, data cleaning, or report writing.
- The problem is already fully formulated and the user only asks for code translation into a specific solver; use the solver-specific skill as the primary guide.
- The request is exploratory and the user explicitly wants brainstorming without implementation.

## Model-first workflow

1. Restate the business decision in plain language.
2. Identify sets, indices, units, and time granularity.
3. Extract parameters from data files and confirm units.
4. Define decision variables with domains and business meaning.
5. Write the objective with every cost, reward, penalty, and service term.
6. Write constraints grouped by operational rule.
7. Check edge cases: missing eligibility, impossible demand, fixed commitments, and terminal-state requirements.
8. Only then implement code.

## Ambiguity handling

- If the brief is ambiguous but data and examples imply a convention, state the assumption and continue.
- If ambiguity changes feasibility, objective direction, or deliverable schema, ask before coding or implement the least risky interpretation with a clear note.
- If units conflict across files, normalize units explicitly before modeling.
- If a hard requirement conflicts with data, diagnose the conflict before adding slack variables.

## Formulation checklist

- Every row in an input table should either become a parameter, an eligibility rule, or be explicitly irrelevant.
- Every decision variable should appear in at least one binding constraint and usually in the objective.
- Demand balance should use the correct equality or inequality direction; do not weaken equality to `>=` unless surplus is genuinely allowed.
- Capacity constraints should include activation/open/setup binaries when the business says inactive resources cannot produce, ship, or serve.
- Shortage, backlog, overtime, outsourcing, and postponement variables need explicit cost and bounds.
- Multi-period models need state transition equations and initial/terminal states.
- If the user asks for explanation, describe the modeling logic in business terms after solving.

## Constraint boundary rules

- Hard constraints represent physical impossibility, policy mandates, legal requirements, or explicit benchmark feasibility rules.
- Soft constraints need penalty variables or objective terms; do not silently turn hard constraints into penalties.
- Service levels should specify numerator, denominator, eligible population, and time window.
- Fairness constraints should define the comparison group and whether unavailable resources count.
- Logical implications need both directions only when the business rule says equivalence, not merely implication.

## Objective boundary rules

- Confirm whether the problem minimizes cost, maximizes profit, maximizes service, or uses lexicographic priorities.
- Do not mix penalties with primary economics unless their scale is intentional.
- Include fixed costs only once per activated entity.
- Include revenue only for fulfilled demand or accepted jobs, not for unmet or canceled work.
- Keep reporting metrics separate from optimized objective terms unless the prompt explicitly optimizes them.

## Implementation expectations

- Use structured parsing for CSV/JSON instead of manual string manipulation.
- Keep solver code, solution export, and validation code clearly separated.
- For benchmark or task environments, read the provided brief before coding and follow the expected output schema exactly.
- When comparing two answers, diagnose concrete formulation differences and objective consequences, not only feasibility.
- Write exported solutions at the precision expected by the checker; avoid unnecessary rounding.
- Preserve the input schema and output schema unless the user asks to redesign the benchmark.

## Verification boundary

- Verify feasibility independently from solver status when an evaluator or checker exists.
- Recompute objective from exported artifacts, not only from in-memory solver expressions.
- For benchmark tasks, create at least one negative check for each newly added rule when practical.
- For paper or real-world tasks, separate what is directly supported by the source from synthetic or assumed data.

## Red flags

- Objective terms that are counted twice or charged at the wrong marginal rate.
- Binary variables not linked tightly enough to continuous activity.
- Time periods shifted by one index.
- Average or fairness constraints that ignore the denominator or eligibility set.
- Public prompt files that reveal solver-ready variables or formulas when the benchmark is meant to test modeling translation.
- A feasible solution that exploits missing eligibility, free disposal, negative cycles, or unbounded production.
- A model that reports optimality after a time limit, infeasible status, or missing incumbent.

description: Use when building, running, validating, or debugging Python optimization models that use PySCIPOpt and SCIP.

---

# OR PySCIPOpt

## Use when

- The task asks for a Python optimization model using SCIP or `pyscipopt`.
- Existing solver code imports `pyscipopt`, sets SCIP parameters, writes `.lp`/`.mps`, or calls a SCIP-based checker.
- The user asks to run, debug, speed up, validate, or explain a SCIP-based solve.

## Do not use when

- The task only needs a mathematical formulation and no solver-specific code.
- The project clearly uses a different solver stack, such as Pyomo with CBC/HiGHS, OR-Tools CP-SAT, PuLP, CPLEX, or cvxpy, unless the user asks to port it to PySCIPOpt.
- The model is nonlinear or stochastic in a way that SCIP cannot handle directly; first decide whether it can be linearized or approximated.

## Environment rule

Use the Python environment specified by the user or the project. In this ORClaw workspace, the default solver environment is the `or` conda environment with PySCIPOpt available:

```bash
conda run -n or python <script.py>
```

If `pyscipopt` is missing, report the missing dependency, identify the command that failed, and try the project-specified environment before changing solver stacks.

## Workflow

1. Read the task brief and input files before coding.
2. Write the full mathematical model first: sets, parameters, decision variables, objective, and constraints.
3. Implement the model with explicit tuple keys and named constraints.
4. Solve with the selected Python command.
5. Export the solution using the exact schema required by the task.
6. Validate with the task checker or an independent recomputation of feasibility and objective.
7. If the solution is not accepted, diagnose whether the issue is formulation, data parsing, export formatting, tolerance, or evaluator mismatch before changing the objective or constraints.

## PySCIPOpt patterns

- Use `from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum`.
- Create sparse variable dictionaries explicitly, for example:

```python
x = {(i, j, t): model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{i},{j},{t}]") for i, j, t in keys}
```

- Use `quicksum(...)` for large linear expressions.
- Use `model.addCons(expr <= rhs, name="capacity[k,t]")` for constraints.
- Use `model.setObjective(expr, "minimize")` or `model.setObjective(expr, "maximize")`.
- Set reproducibility and limits with SCIP parameters:

```python
model.setParam("limits/gap", 0.0005)
model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
```

- After `model.optimize()`, inspect `model.getStatus()` and only read values if a solution exists.
- Read variable values with `model.getVal(x[key])`, not `x[key].X`.
- Read objective and bound with `model.getObjVal()` and `model.getDualbound()`.
- Read relative gap with `model.getGap()` when available.

## Modeling hygiene

- Calibrate every big-M from data. If a tight M is not obvious, derive a bound and document it in a short comment.
- Keep feasibility checks, objective recomputation, and reference-optimality comparison separate.
- Do not round exported decision values unless the schema explicitly requires it.
- For benchmark work, treat the evaluator or checker as the scoring authority and the reference solver as a validation baseline.
- Keep solver logs or a concise solve summary when the user needs auditability.
- Avoid changing data to make a model feasible unless the task is explicitly to design or repair an instance.

## Debugging boundaries

- If a model is infeasible, inspect hard lower bounds, conservation equations, and mutually exclusive binary logic before relaxing constraints.
- If objective values differ, recompute each objective component from exported decisions.
- If runtime is high, first remove dominated variables, tighten big-M values, add eligibility filters, and check whether binaries are really necessary.
- If SCIP returns an incumbent with a gap, say so clearly; do not call it exact optimality unless the status and gap support that claim.
- If an evaluator rejects a solution that the model considers feasible, replay the rejected rule directly from the exported file before changing solver code.

## Useful commands

```bash
python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
conda run -n or python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
<python-command> <solver-script.py>
<python-command> <checker-script.py> --solution <solution-file> --env-dir <task-dir>
```

# Aerospace Composite Autoclave Scheduling

An aerospace composite shop must schedule prepreg parts into autoclave cure cycles over the next four days. Each part has already finished layup or will finish soon; once released from the freezer and layup area, it has a limited out-time before quality risk becomes unacceptable. Autoclaves are expensive bottleneck machines, and the shop wants a feasible, high-quality cure plan that protects due dates while avoiding unnecessary peak-power, cleanup, cooling-lane, and inspection exposure.

## Planning Goal

Create autoclave batches and place every part in exactly one batch. Each batch uses a single autoclave and a single cure recipe. A batch may contain multiple parts if they share that recipe, respect recipe-level mix limits, and all fit inside the autoclave chamber. The objective is to minimize total operating cost, including weighted lateness, electricity cost, resin-family cleanup exposure, post-cure inspection congestion, and a soft penalty for using too much of a part's allowable out-time.

Plan a four-day cure schedule for an aerospace composite shop. Inspect every table under `data/`, write your model explanation to `submissions/model.md`, implement your solver in `submissions/solve.py`, write the schedule to `submissions/solution.json`, and summarize the run in `submissions/solve_log.md`.


## Hard Operating Rules

- Every part in the planning packet must be assigned to one and only one cure batch.
- A part cannot enter cure before it has been released by upstream work, and it cannot remain waiting once its out-time allowance is exhausted.
- All parts in a batch must follow the same cure program.
- A selected autoclave must be qualified to run the cure program assigned to that batch.
- Autoclave selection must also respect each part's material thickness and any enhanced uniformity requirement.
- Part rectangles must be placed inside the selected chamber without overlap. A part may be turned only when its manufacturing instructions allow that orientation change.
- A batch cannot require more vacuum service or thermocouple instrumentation than the selected autoclave can provide.
- Vacuum connections must be feasible on the chamber side where each part is placed, not only in aggregate across the whole machine.
- Each cure program has its own batching policy for load size, usable chamber area, cross-program mixing, coupon-panel handling, cooling, and inspection; every batch must satisfy the applicable policy.
- Autoclave batches may not overlap machine downtime or another batch already assigned to the same autoclave.
- When consecutive batches on the same autoclave switch resin families, the schedule must leave the required cleanup interval before the next cure begins.
- Fixture pools are shared across active batches. A part occupies one fixture of its required type from the beginning to the end of its batch.
- Loading crews are limited during the entire loading operation for each batch, including both the fixed setup effort and the additional work driven by the parts in that load.
- Power-management rules may limit how many autoclaves can be actively curing during a tariff period.
- After unloading, every batch occupies a cooling lane for its required cooling interval, and the shop's cooling-lane capacity may not be exceeded.
- Batches containing parts that require nondestructive testing consume inspection capacity immediately after cooling for the full required inspection interval, including any extra coupon-panel allowance.
- Parts with edge placement preferences should touch a chamber edge; parts with center placement preferences should be placed away from all chamber edges.
- Each program must meet its stated interim service commitment by completing the required share of its parts before the commitment time.

## Data Dictionary

- `config.json`: planning horizon, objective weights, coupon-panel inspection allowance, and units.
- `parts.csv`: part dimensions, cure recipe, fixture type, release time, out-time limit, due time, priority weight, port demands, NDT/coupon/cooling attributes, and placement preference.
- `autoclaves.csv`: chamber dimensions, port capacities, energy draw, and loading crew skill.
- `recipes.csv`: cure duration, loading/unloading time, resin family, and energy multiplier.
- `autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv`: recipe eligibility, processing multiplier, thickness limit, and ultra-uniform capability by autoclave.
- `recipe_batch_rules.csv`: recipe-level batch size, usable chamber-area, program mixing, coupon, cooling, and inspection rules.
- `vacuum_manifold_limits.csv`: side-specific vacuum manifold capacities by autoclave.
- `fixtures.csv`: shared fixture-pool capacities.
- `setup_times.csv`: resin-family cleanup time required between consecutive batches on the same autoclave.
- `maintenance_windows.csv`: unavailable intervals for each autoclave.
- `operator_calendar.csv`: loading-crew capacity over time by skill.
- `energy_tariffs.csv`: energy prices and simultaneous active-autoclave limits by time window.
- `cooling_lane_capacity.csv`: cooling-lane capacity after autoclave unload.
- `post_cure_inspection_capacity.csv`: NDT inspection capacity after cooling for batches that contain inspected parts.
- `program_commitments.csv`: family-level service commitments.

## Submission Schema

Write `submissions/solution.json` with this shape:

```json
{
  "batches": [
    {
      "batch_id": "B001",
      "autoclave_id": "AC1",
      "recipe_id": "R_STD_EPOXY",
      "start_min": 125.0,
      "parts": [
        {"part_id": "P001", "x_cm": 0.0, "y_cm": 0.0, "rotated": false}
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

`start_min` is minutes from Monday 06:00. Coordinates are centimeters from the lower-left corner of the selected autoclave chamber. The evaluator derives each batch's duration from the recipe, selected autoclave, and number/area of included parts; do not submit a separate end time.

result58. Command output
---
name: advanced-mip-patterns
description: Use when a task involves fixed charges, logical implications, min/max choices, sequencing, no-overlap, piecewise costs, routing-state logic, or other advanced MIP modeling patterns.
metadata:
  short-description: Advanced MIP formulation patterns
---

# Advanced MIP Patterns

## Use when

- The formulation needs logical implications, fixed charges, activation decisions, ordering, no-overlap, disjunctions, min/max, absolute values, tiers, route state, or resource calendars.
- A simple LP/MIP is producing unrealistic behavior because decisions are not linked tightly enough.
- The model is slow or infeasible and the likely cause is weak big-M logic, excessive pairwise variables, or missing state transitions.

## Do not use when

- A continuous LP is sufficient and adding binaries would not represent a real decision.
- The task is better handled by a specialized CP-SAT, constraint programming, nonlinear, or simulation method and the user did not require MIP.
- The user only needs a high-level explanation; do not introduce advanced constructs unless they clarify the actual model.

## General boundary rules

- Add binaries only for real yes/no choices, disjunctions, or nonconvex structure.
- Prefer the tightest formulation that matches the business rule; avoid decorative binaries.
- Use solver-native constructs when they improve clarity and are supported by the target environment.
- If a construct makes the model much slower, compare it with a simpler valid alternative before keeping it.

## Fixed charge and activation

- Use a binary `y` for whether an activity/resource is active.
- Link activity with a tight bound: `x <= U * y`.
- Add lower activation only when the business requires minimum use: `x >= L * y`.
- Charge fixed cost once through `fixed_cost * y`, not per unit.
- Do not force `y = 1` whenever `x = 0` unless the activity is mandatory.
- If there are startup/shutdown costs over time, separate active state from transition state.

## Logical rules

- Prefer solver-native indicator constraints for simple if-then rules when they keep the model clearer.
- If using big-M, derive M from data bounds and keep one M per rule family when possible.
- For either-or choices, use binaries whose sum is exactly one or at most one, matching the business rule.
- Distinguish implication (`A -> B`) from equivalence (`A <-> B`); the reverse direction often needs a separate constraint.
- For conditional capacity, link both activity and resource use to the condition.

## Min, max, absolute value

- For `z = max(a, b)`, add `z >= a`, `z >= b`, and objective pressure if minimizing z. Use binaries only when equality is needed without objective pressure.
- For absolute deviation `d = |x - target|`, add `d >= x - target` and `d >= target - x`.
- For service-level penalties, separate hard feasibility from soft penalty variables.
- For `min`, either transform with signs or add binaries when equality is not enforced by the objective.
- For ratio constraints, cross-multiply only when denominators are constant or safely bounded away from zero.

## Sequencing and no-overlap

- For jobs sharing a resource, use order binary `before[i,j]`.
- Typical pattern:
  - `start[j] >= end[i] - M * (1 - before[i,j])`
  - `start[i] >= end[j] - M * before[i,j]`
- Compute M from the scheduling horizon, not from an arbitrary large number.
- Include setup, cleanup, travel, rest, or turnover time in the correct side of the ordering constraint.
- Create pairwise order variables only for pairs that can share a resource or conflict.
- If time is discrete and the horizon is small, a time-indexed model may be cleaner than pairwise disjunctions.

## Piecewise and tiers

- Use solver-native piecewise-linear features for convex piecewise costs when suitable.
- For nonconvex tiers, use explicit segment variables and segment activation binaries.
- Ensure tier quantities sum to the original activity and each tier has its own upper bound.
- Distinguish incremental tiers from all-units tiers; their formulations and costs differ.
- Check whether discount tiers create nonconvex incentives that require binary selection.

## State and inventory logic

- State variables need initial conditions, transition equations, and terminal requirements if the business specifies them.
- Route or schedule state should be advanced in event order, not checked only row by row.
- Recovery, restock, cleaning, cooldown, maintenance, and rest rules often require cumulative state or last-event logic.
- If state can reset, model the reset trigger and its resource/time cost.

## Debugging advanced models

- First solve a relaxed or smaller instance and inspect whether the intended binaries activate.
- Add named constraints so IIS output points to business rules.
- If the reference solution is infeasible, replay the relevant state transitions manually before changing the evaluator.
- If the LP relaxation is very weak, inspect big-M values, missing lower/upper links, and symmetry.
- If the model is unexpectedly infeasible, test each advanced rule family on a tiny instance with a known feasible solution.

import csv, json, math
from collections import Counter,defaultdict
parts=list(csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv')))
autos=list(csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv')))
elig=list(csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')))
recipes={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipes.csv'))}
by_recipe=Counter(p['recipe_id'] for p in parts)
by_program=Counter(p['program_id'] for p in parts)
by_recipe_prog=Counter((p['recipe_id'],p['program_id']) for p in parts)
print('parts',len(parts))
print('recipes',by_recipe)
print('programs',by_program)
print('recipe-program top',sorted(by_recipe_prog.items()))
# eligibility counts after thickness/uniform
emap=defaultdict(list)
for e in elig:
    emap[e['recipe_id']].append(e)
for rid in recipes:
    feasible=0
    for p in parts:
        if p['recipe_id']"'!=rid: continue
        cnt=0
        for e in emap[rid]:
            if float(p['"'thickness_mm'])<=float(e['max_thickness_mm']) and (int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])<=int(e['supports_ultra_uniform'])):
                cnt+=1
        print('part',p['part_id'],rid,'eligible_autos',cnt)

I’m checking whether the obvious recipe-program batches are physically packable on the eligible autoclaves before I commit to that higher-level decomposition.
tool74. Command (exit 0)
/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import csv,itertools,math
from collections import defaultdict
parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autos={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
elig=defaultdict(dict)
for e in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')):
    elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}
vm=defaultdict(dict)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/vacuum_manifold_limits.csv')):
    vm[r['autoclave_id']][r['side']]=int(r['max_vacuum_ports'])

def dims(p,rot):
    w,h=float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])
    return (h,w) if rot else (w,h)

def shelf_pack(rects,W,H,require_edge_outer=False):
    # rects: list of (id,w,h,edge_required,center_required)
    # brute-force over orders with FFD shelves; enough for diagnostics
    best=None
    for order in [sorted(rects,key=lambda x:(-x[2],-x[1],x[0]))]:
        shelves=[]
        coords={}
        ok=True
        for pid,w,h,edge,center in order:
            placed=False
            for sh in shelves:
                y,row_h,used=sh
                if h<=row_h+1e-9 and used+w<=W+1e-9:
                    x=used
                    if center and (x<=1e-9 or y<=1e-9 or x+w>=W-1e-9 or y+h>=H-1e-9):
                        pass
                    else:
                        coords[pid]=(x,y)
                        sh[2]+=w
                        placed=True
                        break
            if placed: continue
            y=sum(sh[1] for sh in shelves)
            if y+h>H+1e-9:
                ok=False; break
            x=0
            if center and (x<=1e-9 or y<=1e-9 or x+w>=W-1e-9 or y+h>=H-1e-9):
                # try offset 1 and reserve width
                x=1.0
                if x+w>W-1e-9 or y<=1e-9 or y+h>=H-1e-9:
                    ok=False; break
                shelves.append([y,h,x+w])
                coords[pid]=(x,y)
            else:
                shelves.append([y,h,w])
                coords[pid]=(x,y)
            placed=True
        if ok:
            return True,coords
    return False,None

def test_group(group,aid):
    A=autos[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); H=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    rule=rules[group[0]['recipe_id']]
    area=sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in group)
    if area>float(rule['max_area_fraction'])*W*H+1e-9: return False,'area'
    if len(group)>int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']): return False,'count'
    if sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in group)>int(rule['max_coupon_panels']): return False,'coupon'
    if len(set(p['program_id'] for p in group))>int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']): return False,'program'
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in group)>int(A['vacuum_ports']): return False,'vac_tot'
    if sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in group)>int(A['thermocouple_ports']): return False,'tc'
    for p in group:
        e=elig[p['recipe_id']].get(aid)
        if not e: return False,'recipe'
        if float(p['thickness_mm'])>float(e['max_thickness_mm'])+1e-9: return False,'thickness'
        if int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])>int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']): return False,'uniform'
    ids=[p['part_id'] for p in group]
    # enumerate rotations and side assignments, pack each side with simple shelf
    rot_opts=[]
    for p in group:
        rot_opts.append([0,1] if int(p['rotate_ok']) else [0])
    for rots in itertools.product(*rot_opts):
        wh={p['part_id']:dims(p,bool(r)) for p,r in zip(group,rots)}
        if any(w>half+1e-9 or h>H+1e-9 for w,h in wh.values()):
            continue
        for sides in itertools.product([0,1], repeat=len(group)): # 0 left 1 right
            lv=sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p,s in zip(group,sides) if s==0)
            rv=sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p,s in zip(group,sides) if s==1)
            if lv>vm[aid]['left'] or rv>vm[aid]['right']: continue
            left=[]; right=[]
            for p,s in zip(group,sides):
                w,h=wh[p['part_id']]
                edge=p['preferred_zone']=='edge'; center=p['preferred_zone']=='center'
                (left if s==0 else right).append((p['part_id'],w,h,edge,center))
            okL,_=shelf_pack(left,half,H)
            okR,_=shelf_pack(right,half,H)
            if okL and okR:
                return True,'ok'
    return False,'pack'

by=defaultdict(list)
for p in parts.values():
    by[(p['recipe_id'],p['program_id'])].append(p)
for key,grp in sorted(by.items()):
    feas=[]
    for aid in autos:
        ok,why=test_group(grp,aid)
        print(key,aid,ok,why)
        if ok: feas.append(aid)
    print('feasible autos',feas)
PY"

from collections import defaultdict, Counter

# data
parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autoclaves={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
recipes={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipes.csv'))}

for e in elig_rows: elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}

EPS=1e-6

# simple exact-enough side/shelf layout search

def oriented_dims(p,rot):
    w,h=float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])
    return (h,w) if rot else (w,h)

def shelf_pack(items, W, H, side='left'):
    # items list of dicts: id,w,h,edge,center
    order=sorted(items, key=lambda it:(it['edge'], it['center'], -it['h'], -it['w'], it['id']), reverse=True)
    shelves=[]
    coords={}
    for it in order:
        placed=False
        # try existing shelves first
        for sh in shelves:
            y, row_h, used = sh['y'], sh['h'], sh['used']
            if it['h'] <= row_h + EPS and used + it['w'] <= W + EPS:
                x_local = used
                if it['edge']:
                    x_local = 0.0 if side=='left' else W-it['w']
                    if abs(x_local-used) > 1e-9:
                        continue
                if it['center'] and (x_local <= EPS or y <= EPS or x_local + it['w'] >= W-EPS or y + it['h'] >= H-EPS):
                    # allow centered item only in new interior shelf
                    continue
                coords[it['id']] = (x_local, y)
                sh['used'] = max(used + it['w'], x_local + it['w'])
                placed=True
                break
        if placed:
            continue
        y = sum(sh['h'] for sh in shelves)
        if y + it['h'] > H + EPS:
            return None
        x_local = 0.0
        if it['center']:
            x_local = 1.0
            if x_local + it['w'] >= W - EPS or y <= EPS or y + it['h'] >= H - EPS:
                return None
        if it['edge']:
            x_local = 0.0 if side=='left' else W - it['w']
        coords[it['id']] = (x_local, y)
        shelves.append({'y': y, 'h': it['h'], 'used': max(it['w'], x_local + it['w'])})
    return coords

layout_cache={}

def find_layout(batch, aid):
    key=(batch['id'], aid)
    if key in layout_cache: return layout_cache[key]
    A=autoclaves[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); H=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2.0
    grp=[parts[pid] for pid in batch['parts']]
    rid=batch['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]
    area=sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in grp)
    if area > float(rule['max_area_fraction']) * W * H + EPS:
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    if len(grp)>int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']) or sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)>int(rule['max_coupon_panels']) or len({p['program_id'] for p in grp})>int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']):
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in grp) > int(A['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in grp) > int(A['thermocouple_ports']):
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    for p in grp:
        e=elig[rid].get(aid)
        if not e or float(p['thickness_mm']) > float(e['max_thickness_mm']) + EPS or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform']) > int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']):
            layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    rot_opts=[]
    for p in grp:
        rot_opts.append([0,1] if int(p['rotate_ok']) else [0])
    for rots in itertools.product(*rot_opts):
        dims={p['part_id']: oriented_dims(p, bool(r)) for p,r in zip(grp,rots)}
        if any(w > half + EPS or h > H + EPS for (w,h) in dims.values()):
            continue
        for sides in itertools.product(['left','right'], repeat=len(grp)):
            lv=sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p,s in zip(grp,sides) if s=='left')
            rv=sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p,s in zip(grp,sides) if s=='right')
            if lv > vm[aid]['left'] or rv > vm[aid]['right']:
                continue
            left=[]; right=[]
            for p,s in zip(grp,sides):
                w,h=dims[p['part_id']]
                left_right={'id':p['part_id'],'w':w,'h':h,'edge':p['preferred_zone']=='edge','center':p['preferred_zone']=='center'}
                (left if s=='left' else right).append(left_right)
            coordsL=shelf_pack(left, half, H, 'left')
            if coordsL is None: continue
            coordsR=shelf_pack(right, half, H, 'right')
            if coordsR is None: continue
            out=[]
            for p,s,r in zip(grp,sides,rots):
                x,y = coordsL[p['part_id']] if s=='left' else coordsR[p['part_id']]
                if s=='right':
                    x += half
                out.append({'part_id':p['part_id'],'x_cm':x,'y_cm':y,'rotated':bool(r)})
            layout_cache[key]=out
            return out
    layout_cache[key]=None
    return None

# durations
cand={}  # batch -> autoclave data
for b in batches:
    grp=[parts[pid] for pid in b['parts']]
    n=len(grp)

    release=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in grp)
    latest=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in grp)
    due=min(float(p['due_min']) for p in grp)
    b.update({'release':release,'latest':latest,'due':due,'count':n,'ndt_count':ndt,'coupon_count':coupons,'fixture_counts':Counter(p['fixture_type'] for p in grp)})
    for aid in autoclaves:
        layout=find_layout(b,aid)
        if layout is None: continue
        rec=recipes[b['recipe_id']]; e=elig[b['recipe_id']][aid]; rule=rules[b['recipe_id']]
        load=float(rec['load_fixed_min'])+float(rec['load_per_part_min'])*n
        cure=float(rec['cure_min'])*float(e['process_multiplier'])
        unload=float(rec['unload_min'])
        dur=load+cure+unload
        cool=float(rule['cooling_fixed_min'])+float(rule['cooling_per_part_min'])*n
        insp=(float(rule['ndt_minutes_fixed'])+float(rule['ndt_minutes_per_part'])*ndt+float(config['coupon_panel_inspection_minutes'])*coupons) if ndt>0 else 0.0
        cand[(b['id'],aid)]={'layout':layout,'load':load,'cure':cure,'unload':unload,'dur':dur,'cool':cool,'insp':insp}

print('batches',[(b['id'],b['recipe_id'],b['program_id'],b['count'],sorted(a for (bid,a) in cand if bid==b['id'])) for b in batches])

# validator helpers

def interval_overlap(a,b,c,d):
    return max(a,c) < min(b,d) - 1e-9

def cap_at(intervals,t):
    for s,e,c in intervals:
        if s <= t < e:
            return c
    return None

def validate(sol, verbose=False):
    # batch fields
    if len(sol)"'!=len(batches): return False,'"'batch_count'
    ids=set()
    part_seen={}
    by_auto=defaultdict(list)
    fix_events=defaultdict(list)
    load_events=defaultdict(list)
    cure_events=[]
    cool_events=[]
    insp_events=[]
    prog_done=defaultdict(int)
    for entry in sol:
        b=next(bb for bb in batches if bb['id']==entry['id'])
        aid=entry['autoclave_id']; t=entry['start']
        if entry['id'] in ids: return False,'dup_batch'
        ids.add(entry['id'])
        c=cand[(b['id'],aid)]
        rec=recipes[b['recipe_id']]
        end=t+c['dur']
        cure_s=t+c['load']; cure_e=cure_s+c['cure']
        cool_s=end; cool_e=end+c['cool']
        insp_s=cool_e; insp_e=cool_e+c['insp']
        if t < b['release']-1e-9 or t > b['latest']+1e-9 or end > H+1e-9: return False,(b['id'],'timewindow',t,b['release'],b['latest'],end)
        for mw in maint[aid]:
            if interval_overlap(t,end,*mw): return False,(b['id'],'maint',aid,mw)
        for pid in b['parts']:
            if pid in part_seen: return False,'dup_part'
            part_seen[pid]=b['id']
        by_auto[aid].append((t,end,b,c))
        for ft,qty in b['fixture_counts'].items():
            fix_events[ft].append((t,end,qty))
        skill=autoclaves[aid]['operator_skill']
        load_events[skill].append((t,t+c['load'],1))
        cure_events.append((cure_s,cure_e,1))
        cool_events.append((cool_s,cool_e,1))
        if c['insp']>0:
            insp_events.append((insp_s,insp_e,1))
        if end <= next(float(rr['commitment_due_min']) for rr in commit if rr['program_id']==b['program_id']) + 1e-9:
            prog_done[b['program_id']] += len(b['parts'])
    if set(part_seen)"'!=set(parts): return False,'"'missing_parts'
    # machine + setup
    for aid,lst in by_auto.items():
        lst.sort()
        for i,(s,e,b,c) in enumerate(lst):
            if i:
                ps,pe,pb,pc = lst[i-1]
                gap=setup[(recipes[pb['recipe_id']]['resin_family'], recipes[b['recipe_id']]['resin_family'])]
                if s < pe + gap - 1e-9: return False,(aid,'setup',pb['id'],b['id'],pe,gap,s)
    def check_capacity(events, cap_segments, name):
        points=sorted({0.0,H} | {x for s,e,_ in events for x in (s,e)} | {x for s,e,*_ in cap_segments for x in (s,e)})
        for l,r in zip(points, points[1:]):
            if r-l <= 1e-9: continue
            m=(l+r)/2
            usage=sum(v for s,e,v in events if s <= m < e)
            cap=cap_at(cap_segments,m)
            if cap is None: continue
            if usage > cap + 1e-9:
                return False,(name,l,r,usage,cap)
        return True,None
    # exact cap tables
    ok,msg=check_capacity(cure_events, energy, 'energy')
    if not ok: return False,msg
    ok,msg=check_capacity(cool_events, cooling, 'cooling')
    if not ok: return False,msg
    ok,msg=check_capacity(insp_events, inspect, 'inspection')
    if not ok: return False,msg
    for skill,events in load_events.items():
        ok,msg=check_capacity(events, ops[skill], f'load_{skill}')
        if not ok: return False,msg
    for ft,events in fix_events.items():
        seg=[(0.0,H,fixtures[ft][0])]
        ok,msg=check_capacity(events, seg, f'fixture_{ft}')
        if not ok: return False,msg
    # commitments
    for rr in commit:
        req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction']) * sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id']) - 1e-9)
        if prog_done[rr['program_id']] < req:
            return False,('commit',rr['program_id'],prog_done[rr['program_id']],req)
    return True,'ok'
