import csv,itertools
from collections import defaultdict
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autoclaves={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
elig=defaultdict(dict)
for e in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')): elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}
vm=defaultdict(dict)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/vacuum_manifold_limits.csv')): vm[r['autoclave_id']][r['side']]=int(r['max_vacuum_ports'])

def pack(grp, aid):
    A=autoclaves[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); H=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    rid=grp[0]['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]
    if sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in grp) > float(rule['max_area_fraction'])*W*H + 1e-9: return 'area'
    if len(grp)>int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']) or sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)>int(rule['max_coupon_panels']) or len({p['program_id'] for p in grp})>int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']): return 'batchrule'
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in grp)>int(A['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in grp)>int(A['thermocouple_ports']): return 'ports'
    for p in grp:
        e=elig[rid].get(aid)
        if not e: return 'recipe'
        if float(p['thickness_mm'])>float(e['max_thickness_mm'])+1e-9 or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])>int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']): return 'elig'
    m=Model()
    m.setParam('display/verblevel',0)
    m.setParam('limits/time',10)
    x={}; y={}; r={}; left={}; right={}; w={}; h={}
    for p in grp:
        pid=p['part_id']
        x[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0, ub=W, name=f'x[{pid}]')
        y[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0, ub=H, name=f'y[{pid}]')
        if int(p['rotate_ok']):
            r[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'r[{pid}]')
        else:
            r[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', lb=0, ub=0, name=f'r[{pid}]')
        left[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'l[{pid}]')
        right[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'rr[{pid}]')
        m.addCons(left[pid]+right[pid]==1)
        w0=float(p['width_cm']); h0=float(p['height_cm'])
        w[pid]=w0 + (h0-w0)*r[pid]
        h[pid]=h0 + (w0-h0)*r[pid]
        m.addCons(x[pid]+w[pid] <= W)
        m.addCons(y[pid]+h[pid] <= H)
        m.addCons(x[pid]+w[pid] <= half + W*(1-left[pid]))
        m.addCons(x[pid] >= half - W*(1-right[pid]))
        pref=p['preferred_zone']
        if pref=='center':
            m.addCons(x[pid] >= 1e-3)
            m.addCons(y[pid] >= 1e-3)
            m.addCons(x[pid]+w[pid] <= W-1e-3)
            m.addCons(y[pid]+h[pid] <= H-1e-3)
        elif pref=='edge':
            eL=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'eL[{pid}]')
            eR=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'eR[{pid}]')
            eB=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'eB[{pid}]')
            eT=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'eT[{pid}]')
            m.addCons(eL+eR+eB+eT >= 1)
            m.addCons(x[pid] <= W*(1-eL))
            m.addCons(x[pid]+w[pid] >= W - W*(1-eR))
            m.addCons(y[pid] <= H*(1-eB))
            m.addCons(y[pid]+h[pid] >= H - H*(1-eT))
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*left[p['part_id']] for p in grp) <= vm[aid]['left'])
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*right[p['part_id']] for p in grp) <= vm[aid]['right'])
    Mx=W; My=H
    ids=[p['part_id'] for p in grp]
    for i in range(len(ids)):
        for j in range(i+1,len(ids)):
            a,b=ids[i],ids[j]
            L=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'L[{a},{b}]')
            R=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'R[{a},{b}]')
            B=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'B[{a},{b}]')
            T=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'T[{a},{b}]')
            m.addCons(L+R+B+T >= 1)
            m.addCons(x[a]+w[a] <= x[b] + Mx*(1-L))
            m.addCons(x[b]+w[b] <= x[a] + Mx*(1-R))
            m.addCons(y[a]+h[a] <= y[b] + My*(1-B))
            m.addCons(y[b]+h[b] <= y[a] + My*(1-T))
    m.setObjective(0)
    m.optimize()
    return m.getStatus()

for key in [('R_RADOME_LOW','Radar_upgrade'),('R_RADOME_LOW','UAV_block4'),('R_TOUGHENED','Radar_upgrade'),('R_TOUGHENED','UAV_block4')]:
    grp=by[key]
    print(key)
    for aid in autoclaves:
        print(' ',aid,pack(grp,aid))

assistant85. Assistant message
The first batch design exposed the real feasibility issue: some parts in the same recipe-program group have disjoint feasible start windows, so they cannot share a cure batch. I’m rebuilding the batch partition around intersection of part start windows rather than just recipe/program.
assistant86. Assistant message
The feasibility search is running with the revised batch partition. I’m waiting on the exact layout checks and greedy schedule build before deciding whether I need a repair operator.
assistant87. Assistant message
The packing phase is heavier than expected because it’s solving a layout MIP for every candidate batch-autoclave pair. If this keeps stalling, I’ll switch the search operator and replace most of those calls with a cheaper exact-enough constructive layout plus MIP only on hard cases.

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import csv, json, math
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum

# ---------- data ----------
parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autoclaves={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
recipes={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipes.csv'))}
elig=defaultdict(dict)
for e in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')): elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}
vm=defaultdict(dict)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/vacuum_manifold_limits.csv')): vm[r['autoclave_id']][r['side']]=int(r['max_vacuum_ports'])
fixtures={r['fixture_type']:(int(r['capacity']),float(r['setup_buffer_min'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/fixtures.csv'))}
setup={(r['from_resin_family'],r['to_resin_family']):float(r['setup_min']) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/setup_times.csv'))}
maint=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/maintenance_windows.csv')): maint[r['autoclave_id']].append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min'])))
ops=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/operator_calendar.csv')): ops[r['skill']].append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])))
energy=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['max_active_autoclaves'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/energy_tariffs.csv'))]
cooling=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/cooling_lane_capacity.csv'))]
inspect=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/post_cure_inspection_capacity.csv'))]
commit=[r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/program_commitments.csv'))]
config=json.load(open('/app/data/config.json'))
H=float(config['planning_horizon_min'])
EPS=1e-6

def cap_at(intervals,t):
    for row in intervals:
        s,e,c=row[:3]
        if s <= t < e:
            return c
    return None

def interval_overlap(a,b,c,d):
    return max(a,c) < min(b,d) - 1e-9

# ---------- batch construction ----------
by=defaultdict(list)
for p in parts.values():
    by[(p['recipe_id'],p['program_id'])].append(p)

batches=[]
idx=1
for key,grp in sorted(by.items()):
    grp=sorted(grp,key=lambda p:(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']), float(p['release_min']), float(p['due_min']), p['part_id']))
    cur=[]; rel=-1e18; latest=1e18
    for p in grp:
        r=float(p['release_min']); l=r+float(p['max_out_time_min'])
        nr=max(rel,r); nl=min(latest,l)
        if cur and nr > nl + 1e-9:
            batches.append({'id':f'B{idx:03d}','recipe_id':key[0],'program_id':key[1],'parts':[q['part_id'] for q in cur]})
            idx+=1
            cur=[p]; rel=r; latest=l
        else:
            cur.append(p); rel=nr; latest=nl
    if cur:
        batches.append({'id':f'B{idx:03d}','recipe_id':key[0],'program_id':key[1],'parts':[q['part_id'] for q in cur]})
        idx+=1

# ---------- exact packing feasibility ----------
layout_cache={}

def get_layout(batch, aid):
    key=(batch['id'],aid)
    if key in layout_cache: return layout_cache[key]
    grp=[parts[pid] for pid in batch['parts']]
    A=autoclaves[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); Hh=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    rid=batch['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]
    if sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in grp) > float(rule['max_area_fraction'])*W*Hh + EPS:
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    if len(grp) > int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']) or sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp) > int(rule['max_coupon_panels']) or len({p['program_id'] for p in grp}) > int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']):
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in grp) > int(A['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in grp) > int(A['thermocouple_ports']):
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    for p in grp:
        e=elig[rid].get(aid)
        if not e or float(p['thickness_mm']) > float(e['max_thickness_mm']) + EPS or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform']) > int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']):
            layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    m=Model()
    m.setParam('display/verblevel',0)
    m.setParam('limits/time',15)
    x={}; y={}; rot={}; left={}; right={}; widths={}; heights={}; edge_vars={}
    for p in grp:
        pid=p['part_id']
        x[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0, ub=W, name=f'x[{pid}]')
        y[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0, ub=Hh, name=f'y[{pid}]')
        if int(p['rotate_ok']):
            rot[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'r[{pid}]')
        else:
            rot[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', lb=0, ub=0, name=f'r[{pid}]')
        left[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'l[{pid}]')
        right[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'rr[{pid}]')
        m.addCons(left[pid]+right[pid]==1)
        w0=float(p['width_cm']); h0=float(p['height_cm'])
        widths[pid]=w0 + (h0-w0)*rot[pid]
        heights[pid]=h0 + (w0-h0)*rot[pid]
        m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid] <= W)
        m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid] <= Hh)
        m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid] <= half + W*(1-left[pid]))
        m.addCons(x[pid] >= half - W*(1-right[pid]))
        pref=p['preferred_zone']
        if pref=='center':
            m.addCons(x[pid] >= 1e-3)
            m.addCons(y[pid] >= 1e-3)
            m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid] <= W-1e-3)
            m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid] <= Hh-1e-3)
        elif pref=='edge':
            eL=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'eL[{pid}]')
            eR=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'eR[{pid}]')
            eB=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'eB[{pid}]')
            eT=m.addVar(vtype='B', name=f'eT[{pid}]')
            edge_vars[pid]=(eL,eR,eB,eT)
            m.addCons(eL+eR+eB+eT >= 1)
            m.addCons(x[pid] <= W*(1-eL))
            m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid] >= W - W*(1-eR))
            m.addCons(y[pid] <= Hh*(1-eB))
            m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid] >= Hh - Hh*(1-eT))
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*left[p['part_id']] for p in grp) <= vm[aid]['left'])
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*right[p['part_id']] for p in grp) <= vm[aid]['right'])
    ids=[p['part_id'] for p in grp]
    for i in range(len(ids)):
        for j in range(i+1,len(ids)):
            a,b=ids[i],ids[j]
            L=m.addVar(vtype='B'); R=m.addVar(vtype='B'); B=m.addVar(vtype='B'); T=m.addVar(vtype='B')
            m.addCons(L+R+B+T >= 1)
            m.addCons(x[a]+widths[a] <= x[b] + W*(1-L))
            m.addCons(x[b]+widths[b] <= x[a] + W*(1-R))
            m.addCons(y[a]+heights[a] <= y[b] + Hh*(1-B))
            m.addCons(y[b]+heights[b] <= y[a] + Hh*(1-T))
    # keep parts low/left for a stable layout
    m.setObjective(quicksum(x[pid]+y[pid] for pid in ids), 'minimize')
    m.optimize()
    if m.getStatus() not in ('optimal','timelimit','bestsollimit') or m.getNSols()==0:
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    sol=[]
    s=m.getBestSol()
    for p in grp:
        pid=p['part_id']
        sol.append({'part_id':pid,'x_cm':float(m.getSolVal(s,x[pid])),'y_cm':float(m.getSolVal(s,y[pid])),'rotated':bool(round(m.getSolVal(s,rot[pid])) )})
    layout_cache[key]=sol
    return sol

# ---------- enrich batches ----------
cand={}
for b in batches:
    grp=[parts[pid] for pid in b['parts']]
    b['release']=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in grp)
    b['latest']=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in grp)
    b['due']=min(float(p['due_min']) for p in grp)
    b['fixture_counts']=Counter(p['fixture_type'] for p in grp)
    b['count']=len(grp)
    b['ndt_count']=sum(int(p['ndt_required']) for p in grp)
    b['coupon_count']=sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)
    for aid in autoclaves:
        layout=get_layout(b,aid)
        if layout is None:
            continue
        rec=recipes[b['recipe_id']]
        e=elig[b['recipe_id']][aid]
        rule=rules[b['recipe_id']]
        load=float(rec['load_fixed_min'])+float(rec['load_per_part_min'])*b['count']
        cure=float(rec['cure_min'])*float(e['process_multiplier'])
        unload=float(rec['unload_min'])
        dur=load+cure+unload
        cool=float(rule['cooling_fixed_min'])+float(rule['cooling_per_part_min'])*b['count']
        insp=(float(rule['ndt_minutes_fixed'])+float(rule['ndt_minutes_per_part'])*b['ndt_count']+float(config['coupon_panel_inspection_minutes'])*b['coupon_count']) if b['ndt_count']>0 else 0.0
        cand[(b['id'],aid)]={'layout':layout,'load':load,'cure':cure,'unload':unload,'dur':dur,'cool':cool,'insp':insp}

print('batch summary')
for b in batches:
    feas=sorted(a for (bid,a) in cand if bid==b['id'])
    print(b['id'],b['recipe_id'],b['program_id'],b['parts'],'window',b['release'],b['latest'],'autos',feas)

# ---------- feasibility checks ----------
def partial_feasible(sol):
    by_auto=defaultdict(list)
    fix_events=defaultdict(list)
    load_events=defaultdict(list)
    cure_events=[]
    cool_events=[]
    insp_events=[]
    for ent in sol:
        b=next(bb for bb in batches if bb['id']==ent['id'])
        c=cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]
        s=ent['start']; e=s+c['dur']
        if s < b['release']-1e-9 or s > b['latest']+1e-9 or e > H+1e-9:
            return False,('window',b['id'],s,b['release'],b['latest'],e)
        for mw in maint[ent['autoclave_id']]:
            if interval_overlap(s,e,*mw):
                return False,('maint',b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'],mw)
        by_auto[ent['autoclave_id']].append((s,e,b))
        for ft,qty in b['fixture_counts'].items():
            fix_events[ft].append((s,e,qty))
        load_events[autoclaves[ent['autoclave_id']]['operator_skill']].append((s,s+c['load'],1))
        cure_events.append((s+c['load'],s+c['load']+c['cure'],1))
        cool_events.append((e,e+c['cool'],1))
        if c['insp']>0:
            insp_events.append((e+c['cool'],e+c['cool']+c['insp'],1))
    for aid,lst in by_auto.items():
        lst.sort()
        for i in range(1,len(lst)):
            ps,pe,pb=lst[i-1]
            s,e,b=lst[i]
            gap=setup[(recipes[pb['recipe_id']]['resin_family'], recipes[b['recipe_id']]['resin_family'])]
            if s < pe + gap - 1e-9:
                return False,('setup',aid,pb['id'],b['id'],pe,gap,s)
    def check_capacity(events, cap_segments, name):
        points=sorted({0.0,H} | {x for s,e,_ in events for x in (s,e)} | {x for row in cap_segments for x in row[:2]})
        for l,r in zip(points,points[1:]):
            if r-l <= 1e-9: continue
            m=(l+r)/2.0
            usage=sum(v for s,e,v in events if s <= m < e)
            cap=cap_at(cap_segments,m)
            if cap is not None and usage > cap + 1e-9:
                return False,(name,l,r,usage,cap)
        return True,None
    ok,msg=check_capacity(cure_events, energy, 'energy')
    if not ok: return False,msg
    ok,msg=check_capacity(cool_events, cooling, 'cooling')
    if not ok: return False,msg
    ok,msg=check_capacity(insp_events, inspect, 'inspection')
    if not ok: return False,msg
    for skill,ev in load_events.items():
        ok,msg=check_capacity(ev, ops[skill], f'load_{skill}')
        if not ok: return False,msg
    for ft,ev in fix_events.items():
        ok,msg=check_capacity(ev, [(0.0,H,fixtures[ft][0])], f'fixture_{ft}')
        if not ok: return False,msg
    return True,'ok'

def full_validate(sol):
    ok,msg=partial_feasible(sol)
    if not ok: return ok,msg
    if len(sol)"'!=len(batches): return False,'"'count'
    seen=set()
    prog_done=defaultdict(int)
    for ent in sol:
        b=next(bb for bb in batches if bb['id']==ent['id'])
        if b['id'] in seen: return False,'dup_batch'
        seen.add(b['id'])
        end=ent['start']+cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]['dur']
        if end <= next(float(rr['commitment_due_min']) for rr in commit if rr['program_id']==b['program_id']) + 1e-9:
            prog_done[b['program_id']] += len(b['parts'])
    if seen "'!= {b['"'id'] for b in batches}: return False,'missing_batch'
    for rr in commit:
        total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id'])
        req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total - 1e-9)
        if prog_done[rr['program_id']] < req:
            return False,('commit',rr['program_id'],prog_done[rr['program_id']],req)
    return True,'ok'

# ---------- greedy schedule ----------
orders=[]
base=sorted(batches, key=lambda b:(len([a for (bid,a) in cand if bid==b['id']]), b['latest']-b['release'], b['latest'], b['due']))
orders.append(base)
orders.append(sorted(batches, key=lambda b:(b['latest'], len([a for (bid,a) in cand if bid==b['id']]), b['due'])))
orders.append(sorted(batches, key=lambda b:(next(float(rr['commitment_due_min']) for rr in commit if rr['program_id']==b['program_id']), b['latest'])))
orders.append(sorted(batches, key=lambda b:(b['due'], b['latest'])))

best=None
for oi,order in enumerate(orders):
    sol=[]
    fail=None
    for b in order:
        choices=[]
        for aid in sorted(a for (bid,a) in cand if bid==b['id']):
            c=cand[(b['id'],aid)]
            lb=math.ceil(b['release'])
            ub=math.floor(b['latest'])
            for t in range(lb, ub+1):
                ok,msg=partial_feasible(sol+[{'id':b['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':float(t)}])
                if ok:
                    end=t+c['dur']
                    score=(end, t, aid)
                    choices.append((score,aid,float(t)))
                    break
        if not choices:
            fail=b['id']
            break
        _,aid,t=min(choices)
        sol.append({'id':b['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':t})
    if fail is None:
        ok,msg=full_validate(sol)
        print('order',oi,'result',ok,msg)
        if ok:
            best=sol
            break
        else:
            print('failed final',msg)
    else:
        print('order',oi,'stalled at',fail)

print('BEST',best)
if best:
    for ent in sorted(best,key=lambda x:x['start']):
        b=next(bb for bb in batches if bb['id']==ent['id'])
        c=cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]
        print(ent['id'],b['recipe_id'],b['program_id'],ent['autoclave_id'],ent['start'],ent['start']+c['dur'])

    for p in grp:
        r=float(p['release_min']); l=r+float(p['max_out_time_min'])
        nr=max(rel,r); nl=min(latest,l)

        else:

def dims(p,rot):
    w,h=float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])
    return (h,w) if rot else (w,h)

def feasible_pref(side, Wfull, H, x,y,w,h,pref):
    gx = x if side=='left' else Wfull/2 + x
    if pref=='center':
        return gx > 1e-6 and y > 1e-6 and gx + w < Wfull - 1e-6 and y + h < H - 1e-6
    if pref=='edge':
        return gx <= 1e-6 or y <= 1e-6 or gx + w >= Wfull - 1e-6 or y + h >= H - 1e-6
    return True

def overlaps(x,y,w,h,placed):
    for _,px,py,pw,ph in placed:
        if max(x,px) < min(x+w,px+pw) - 1e-9 and max(y,py) < min(y+h,py+ph) - 1e-9:
            return True
    return False

def pack_side(items, side, Wside, Wfull, H):
    items=sorted(items, key=lambda it:(it['pref']"'!='"'center', -it['h'], -it['w'], it['id']))
    placed=[]
    def rec(k):
        if k==len(items): return True
        it=items[k]
        xs={0.0}
        ys={0.0}
        for _,px,py,pw,ph in placed:
            xs.add(px+pw)
            ys.add(py+ph)
        # extra candidates for right edge touching within side and top edge touching
        xs.add(max(0.0, Wside-it['w']))
        ys.add(max(0.0, H-it['h']))
        for y in sorted(ys):
            for x in sorted(xs):
                if x+it['w'] > Wside + 1e-9 or y+it['h'] > H + 1e-9:
                    continue
                if overlaps(x,y,it['w'],it['h'],placed):
                    continue
                if not feasible_pref(side,Wfull,H,x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['pref']):
                    continue
                placed.append((it['id'],x,y,it['w'],it['h']))
                if rec(k+1): return True
                placed.pop()
        return False
    ok=rec(0)
    if not ok: return None
    return {pid:(x,y,w,h) for pid,x,y,w,h in placed}

def pack_batch(part_ids, aid):
    grp=[parts[pid] for pid in part_ids]
    rid=grp[0]['recipe_id']
    A=autos[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); H=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    rule=rules[rid]
    if sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in grp) > float(rule['max_area_fraction'])*W*H + 1e-9: return False
    if len(grp)>int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']) or sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)>int(rule['max_coupon_panels']) or len({p['program_id'] for p in grp})>int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']): return False
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in grp)>int(A['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in grp)>int(A['thermocouple_ports']): return False
    e=elig[rid].get(aid)
    if not e: return False
    for p in grp:
        if float(p['thickness_mm'])>float(e['max_thickness_mm'])+1e-9 or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])>int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']): return False
    rot_opts=[[0,1] if int(p['rotate_ok']) else [0] for p in grp]
    for rots in itertools.product(*rot_opts):
        rr=[]
        bad=False
        for p,r in zip(grp,rots):
            w,h=dims(p,bool(r))
            if w>half+1e-9 or h>H+1e-9:
                bad=True; break
            rr.append({'id':p['part_id'],'w':w,'h':h,'pref':p['preferred_zone'],'rot':bool(r),'vac':int(p['vacuum_ports'])})
        if bad: continue
        for sides in itertools.product(['left','right'], repeat=len(rr)):
            lv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(rr,sides) if s=='left')
            rv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(rr,sides) if s=='right')
            if lv>vm[aid]['left'] or rv>vm[aid]['right']: continue
            left=[it for it,s in zip(rr,sides) if s=='left']
            right=[it for it,s in zip(rr,sides) if s=='right']
            lp=pack_side(left,'left',half,W,H)
            if lp is None: continue
            rp=pack_side(right,'right',half,W,H)
            if rp is None: continue
            return True
    return False

start=time.time()
for key,part_ids in batches:
    feas=[]
    for aid in autos:
        ok=pack_batch(part_ids, aid)
        print(key,part_ids,aid,ok)
        if ok: feas.append(aid)
    print('feas',feas)
print('elapsed',time.time()-start)

        nr=max(rel,r); nl=min(latest,l)

# helpers

def cap_at(intervals,t):
    for s,e,c in intervals:
        if s <= t < e:
            return c
    return None

def interval_overlap(a,b,c,d):
    return max(a,c) < min(b,d) - 1e-9

def dims(p,rot):
    w,h=float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])
    return (h,w) if rot else (w,h)

def feasible_pref(side, Wfull, Hh, x,y,w,h,pref):
    gx = x if side=='left' else Wfull/2 + x
    if pref=='center':
        return gx > 1e-6 and y > 1e-6 and gx+w < Wfull-1e-6 and y+h < Hh-1e-6
    if pref=='edge':
        return gx <= 1e-6 or y <= 1e-6 or gx+w >= Wfull-1e-6 or y+h >= Hh-1e-6
    return True

def backtrack_pack(items, side, Wside, Wfull, Hh):
    items=sorted(items, key=lambda it:(it['pref']=='edge', it['pref']"'!='"'center', -it['h'], -it['w'], it['id']), reverse=True)
    placed=[]
    def rec(k):
        if k==len(items): return True
        it=items[k]
        xs={0.0, max(0.0, Wside-it['w'])}
        ys={0.0, max(0.0, Hh-it['h'])}
        for _,px,py,pw,ph in placed:
            xs.add(px+pw); ys.add(py+ph)
        for y in sorted(ys):
            for x in sorted(xs):
                if x+it['w'] > Wside+1e-9 or y+it['h'] > Hh+1e-9:
                    continue
                if any(max(x,px)<min(x+it['w'],px+pw)-1e-9 and max(y,py)<min(y+it['h'],py+ph)-1e-9 for _,px,py,pw,ph in placed):
                    continue
                if not feasible_pref(side,Wfull,Hh,x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['pref']):
                    continue
                placed.append((it['id'],x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['rot']))
                if rec(k+1): return True
                placed.pop()
        return False
    if rec(0):
        return {pid:(x,y,w,h,rot) for pid,x,y,w,h,rot in placed}
    return None

mip_cache={}
def mip_layout(batch, aid):
    key=(batch['id'],aid)
    if key in mip_cache: return mip_cache[key]
    grp=[parts[pid] for pid in batch['parts']]
    A=autoclaves[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); Hh=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    rid=batch['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]
    m=Model(); m.setParam('display/verblevel',0); m.setParam('limits/time',4)
    x={}; y={}; rot={}; left={}; right={}; widths={}; heights={}
    for p in grp:
        pid=p['part_id']
        x[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0, ub=W)
        y[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0, ub=Hh)
        rot[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B', lb=0, ub=1 if int(p['rotate_ok']) else 0)
        left[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B'); right[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B')
        m.addCons(left[pid]+right[pid]==1)
        w0=float(p['width_cm']); h0=float(p['height_cm'])
        widths[pid]=w0+(h0-w0)*rot[pid]; heights[pid]=h0+(w0-h0)*rot[pid]
        m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid] <= W)
        m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid] <= Hh)
        m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid] <= half + W*(1-left[pid]))
        m.addCons(x[pid] >= half - W*(1-right[pid]))
        pref=p['preferred_zone']
        if pref=='center':
            m.addCons(x[pid] >= 1e-3); m.addCons(y[pid] >= 1e-3)
            m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid] <= W-1e-3); m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid] <= Hh-1e-3)
        elif pref=='edge':
            eL=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eR=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eB=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eT=m.addVar(vtype='B')
            m.addCons(eL+eR+eB+eT >= 1)
            m.addCons(x[pid] <= W*(1-eL)); m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid] >= W - W*(1-eR))
            m.addCons(y[pid] <= Hh*(1-eB)); m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid] >= Hh - Hh*(1-eT))
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*left[p['part_id']] for p in grp) <= vm[aid]['left'])
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*right[p['part_id']] for p in grp) <= vm[aid]['right'])
    ids=[p['part_id'] for p in grp]
    for i in range(len(ids)):
        for j in range(i+1,len(ids)):
            a,b=ids[i],ids[j]
            L=m.addVar(vtype='B'); R=m.addVar(vtype='B'); B=m.addVar(vtype='B'); T=m.addVar(vtype='B')
            m.addCons(L+R+B+T >= 1)
            m.addCons(x[a]+widths[a] <= x[b] + W*(1-L))
            m.addCons(x[b]+widths[b] <= x[a] + W*(1-R))
            m.addCons(y[a]+heights[a] <= y[b] + Hh*(1-B))
            m.addCons(y[b]+heights[b] <= y[a] + Hh*(1-T))
    m.setObjective(quicksum(x[p]+y[p] for p in ids),'minimize')
    m.optimize()
    if m.getNSols()==0:
        mip_cache[key]=None
        return None
    s=m.getBestSol()
    layout=[]
    for pid in ids:
        layout.append({'part_id':pid,'x_cm':m.getSolVal(s,x[pid]),'y_cm':m.getSolVal(s,y[pid]),'rotated':bool(round(m.getSolVal(s,rot[pid])))})
    mip_cache[key]=layout
    return layout

layout_cache={}
def get_layout(batch, aid):
    key=(batch['id'],aid)
    if key in layout_cache: return layout_cache[key]
    grp=[parts[pid] for pid in batch['parts']]
    A=autoclaves[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); Hh=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    rid=batch['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]
    # easy feasibility
    if sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in grp) > float(rule['max_area_fraction'])*W*Hh + EPS:
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    if len(grp) > int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']) or sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp) > int(rule['max_coupon_panels']) or len({p['program_id'] for p in grp}) > int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']):
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in grp) > int(A['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in grp) > int(A['thermocouple_ports']):
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    e=elig[rid].get(aid)
    if not e:
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    for p in grp:
        if float(p['thickness_mm']) > float(e['max_thickness_mm']) + EPS or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform']) > int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']):
            layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    rot_opts=[[0,1] if int(p['rotate_ok']) else [0] for p in grp]
    for rots in itertools.product(*rot_opts):
        items=[]; bad=False
        for p,r in zip(grp,rots):
            w,h=dims(p,bool(r))
            if w>half+1e-9 or h>Hh+1e-9: bad=True; break
            items.append({'id':p['part_id'],'w':w,'h':h,'pref':p['preferred_zone'],'rot':bool(r),'vac':int(p['vacuum_ports'])})
        if bad: continue
        for sides in itertools.product(['left','right'], repeat=len(items)):
            lv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left')
            rv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right')
            if lv>vm[aid]['left'] or rv>vm[aid]['right']: continue
            left_items=[it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left']
            right_items=[it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right']
            lp=backtrack_pack(left_items,'left',half,W,Hh)
            if lp is None: continue
            rp=backtrack_pack(right_items,'right',half,W,Hh)
            if rp is None: continue
            out=[]
            for it,s in zip(items,sides):
                d=lp[it['id']] if s=='left' else rp[it['id']]
                x,y,w,h,rot=d
                gx=x if s=='left' else half+x
                out.append({'part_id':it['id'],'x_cm':gx,'y_cm':y,'rotated':rot})
            layout_cache[key]=out
            return out
    out=mip_layout(batch,aid)
    layout_cache[key]=out
    return out

# enrich batches
cand={}
for b in batches:
    grp=[parts[pid] for pid in b['parts']]
    b['release']=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in grp)
    b['latest']=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in grp)
    b['due']=min(float(p['due_min']) for p in grp)
    b['fixture_counts']=Counter(p['fixture_type'] for p in grp)
    b['count']=len(grp); b['ndt_count']=sum(int(p['ndt_required']) for p in grp); b['coupon_count']=sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)
    for aid in autoclaves:
        layout=get_layout(b,aid)
        if layout is None: continue
        rec=recipes[b['recipe_id']]; ee=elig[b['recipe_id']][aid]; rule=rules[b['recipe_id']]
        load=float(rec['load_fixed_min']) + float(rec['load_per_part_min']) * b['count']
        cure=float(rec['cure_min']) * float(ee['process_multiplier'])
        unload=float(rec['unload_min'])
        dur=load+cure+unload
        cool=float(rule['cooling_fixed_min']) + float(rule['cooling_per_part_min']) * b['count']
        insp=(float(rule['ndt_minutes_fixed']) + float(rule['ndt_minutes_per_part']) * b['ndt_count'] + float(config['coupon_panel_inspection_minutes']) * b['coupon_count']) if b['ndt_count']>0 else 0.0
        cand[(b['id'],aid)]={'dur':dur,'load':load,'cure':cure,'cool':cool,'insp':insp,'layout':layout}

# partial feasibility
batch_map={b['id']:b for b in batches}
def partial_feasible(sol):
    by_auto=defaultdict(list); fix=defaultdict(list); load_ev=defaultdict(list); cure_ev=[]; cool_ev=[]; insp_ev=[]
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]; c=cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]
        s=ent['start']; e=s+c['dur']
        if s < b['release']-1e-9 or s > b['latest']+1e-9 or e > H+1e-9:
            return False
        for mw in maint[ent['autoclave_id']]:
            if interval_overlap(s,e,*mw): return False
        by_auto[ent['autoclave_id']].append((s,e,b))
        for ft,qty in b['fixture_counts'].items(): fix[ft].append((s,e,qty))
        load_ev[autoclaves[ent['autoclave_id']]['operator_skill']].append((s,s+c['load'],1))
        cure_ev.append((s+c['load'],s+c['load']+c['cure'],1))
        cool_ev.append((e,e+c['cool'],1))
        if c['insp']>0: insp_ev.append((e+c['cool'],e+c['cool']+c['insp'],1))
    for aid,lst in by_auto.items():
        lst.sort()
        for i in range(1,len(lst)):
            ps,pe,pb=lst[i-1]; s,e,b=lst[i]
            gap=setup[(recipes[pb['recipe_id']]['resin_family'], recipes[b['recipe_id']]['resin_family'])]
            if s < pe + gap - 1e-9:
                return False
    def check(events, caps):
        pts=sorted({0.0,H} | {x for s,e,_ in events for x in (s,e)} | {x for s,e,_ in caps for x in (s,e)})
        for l,r in zip(pts,pts[1:]):
            if r-l<=1e-9: continue
            m=(l+r)/2.0
            usage=sum(v for s,e,v in events if s<=m<e)
            cap=cap_at(caps,m)
            if cap is not None and usage > cap + 1e-9: return False
        return True
    if not check(cure_ev, energy): return False
    if not check(cool_ev, cooling): return False
    if not check(insp_ev, inspect): return False
    for skill,ev in load_ev.items():
        if not check(ev, ops[skill]): return False
    for ft,ev in fix.items():
        if not check(ev, [(0.0,H,fixtures[ft][0])]): return False
    return True

def full_validate(sol):
    if len(sol)"'!=len(batches): return False,'"'missing'
    if not partial_feasible(sol): return False,'partial'
    prog_done=defaultdict(int)
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]
        end=ent['start']+cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]['dur']
        if end <= next(float(rr['commitment_due_min']) for rr in commit if rr['program_id']==b['program_id']) + 1e-9:
            prog_done[b['program_id']]+=len(b['parts'])
    for rr in commit:
        total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id'])
        req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total - 1e-9)
        if prog_done[rr['program_id']] < req:
            return False,('commit',rr['program_id'],prog_done[rr['program_id']],req)
    return True,'ok'

# DFS search
for b in batches:
    b['autos']=sorted(a for (bid,a) in cand if bid==b['id'])

choice_cache={}
def choices_for(batch, sol):
    key=(batch['id'], tuple(sorted((e['id'],e['autoclave_id'],e['start']) for e in sol)))
    # not caching massive states for prototype
    out=[]
    for aid in batch['autos']:
        c=cand[(batch['id'],aid)]
        lb=math.ceil(batch['release']); ub=math.floor(batch['latest'])
        found=0
        t=lb
        while t<=ub and found<5:
            ent={'id':batch['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':float(t)}
            if partial_feasible(sol+[ent]):
                out.append((t+c['dur'] + 10*len(batch['autos']) + (batch['latest']-t), aid, float(t)))
                found += 1
                t += 15
            else:
                t += 1
    out.sort()
    return [(aid,t) for _,aid,t in out]

best=None
visited=0
def search(sol, unscheduled):
    global visited,best
    visited += 1
    if time.time()-start_all > 70:
        return False
    if not unscheduled:
        ok,msg=full_validate(sol)
        print('final',ok,msg,'visited',visited)
        if ok:
            best=list(sol)
            return True
        return False
    # choose most constrained batch
    domains=[]
    for b in unscheduled:
        ch=choices_for(b, sol)
        if not ch:
            return False
        domains.append((len(ch), len(b['autos']), b['latest']-b['release'], b['latest'], b, ch))
    domains.sort(key=lambda x:(x[0],x[1],x[2],x[3]))
    _,_,_,_,batch,ch=domains[0]
    rest=[b for b in unscheduled if b['id']"'!=batch['"'id']]
    for aid,t in ch:
        sol.append({'id':batch['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':t})
        if search(sol, rest):
            return True
        sol.pop()
    return False

ok=search([], batches)

print('best',best)

def cap_at(intervals,t):
    for s,e,c in intervals:
        if s<=t<e: return c
    return None

def interval_overlap(a,b,c,d):
    return max(a,c)<min(b,d)-1e-9

def dims(p,rot):
    w,h=float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])
    return (h,w) if rot else (w,h)

def feasible_pref(side,Wfull,Hh,x,y,w,h,pref):
    gx = x if side=='left' else Wfull/2 + x
    if pref=='center':
        return gx>1e-6 and y>1e-6 and gx+w<Wfull-1e-6 and y+h<Hh-1e-6
    if pref=='edge':
        return gx<=1e-6 or y<=1e-6 or gx+w>=Wfull-1e-6 or y+h>=Hh-1e-6
    return True

def backtrack_pack(items, side, Wside, Wfull, Hh):
    items=sorted(items, key=lambda it:(it['pref']=='edge', it['pref']"'!='"'center', -it['h'], -it['w'], it['id']), reverse=True)
    placed=[]
    def rec(k):
        if k==len(items): return True
        it=items[k]
        xs={0.0, max(0.0, Wside-it['w'])}
        ys={0.0, max(0.0, Hh-it['h'])}
        for _,px,py,pw,ph,_ in placed:
            xs.add(px+pw); ys.add(py+ph)
        for y in sorted(ys):
            for x in sorted(xs):
                if x+it['w']>Wside+1e-9 or y+it['h']>Hh+1e-9:
                    continue
                bad=False
                for _,px,py,pw,ph,_ in placed:
                    if max(x,px)<min(x+it['w'],px+pw)-1e-9 and max(y,py)<min(y+it['h'],py+ph)-1e-9:
                        bad=True; break
                if bad: continue
                if not feasible_pref(side,Wfull,Hh,x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['pref']):
                    continue
                placed.append((it['id'],x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['rot']))
                if rec(k+1): return True
                placed.pop()
        return False
    if rec(0):
        return {pid:(x,y,w,h,rot) for pid,x,y,w,h,rot in placed}
    return None

def mip_layout(batch, aid):

    m.setObjective(quicksum(x[p]+y[p] for p in ids),'minimize')

        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    e=elig[rid].get(aid)
    if not e:
        layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    for p in grp:
        if float(p['thickness_mm'])>float(e['max_thickness_mm'])+1e-9 or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])>int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']):
            layout_cache[key]=None; return None
    rot_opts=[[0,1] if int(p['rotate_ok']) else [0] for p in grp]
    for rots in itertools.product(*rot_opts):
        items=[]; bad=False
        for p,r in zip(grp,rots):
            w,h=dims(p,bool(r))
            if w>half+1e-9 or h>Hh+1e-9: bad=True; break
            items.append({'id':p['part_id'],'w':w,'h':h,'pref':p['preferred_zone'],'rot':bool(r),'vac':int(p['vacuum_ports'])})
        if bad: continue
        for sides in itertools.product(['left','right'], repeat=len(items)):
            lv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left')
            rv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right')
            if lv>vm[aid]['left'] or rv>vm[aid]['right']: continue
            left_items=[it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left']
            right_items=[it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right']
            lp=backtrack_pack(left_items,'left',half,W,Hh)
            if lp is None: continue
            rp=backtrack_pack(right_items,'right',half,W,Hh)
            if rp is None: continue
            out=[]
            for it,s in zip(items,sides):
                d=lp[it['id']] if s=='left' else rp[it['id']]
                x,y,w,h,rot=d
                out.append({'part_id':it['id'],'x_cm':x if s=='left' else half+x,'y_cm':y,'rotated':rot})
            layout_cache[key]=out; return out
    out=mip_layout(batch,aid)
    layout_cache[key]=out
    return out

cand={}
for b in batches:
    grp=[parts[pid] for pid in b['parts']]
    b['release']=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in grp)
    b['latest']=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in grp)
    b['due']=min(float(p['due_min']) for p in grp)
    b['fixture_counts']=Counter(p['fixture_type'] for p in grp)
    b['count']=len(grp); b['ndt_count']=sum(int(p['ndt_required']) for p in grp); b['coupon_count']=sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)
    b['autos']=[]
    for aid in autoclaves:
        layout=get_layout(b,aid)
        if layout is None: continue
        b['autos'].append(aid)
        rec=recipes[b['recipe_id']]; ee=elig[b['recipe_id']][aid]; rule=rules[b['recipe_id']]
        load=float(rec['load_fixed_min'])+float(rec['load_per_part_min'])*b['count']
        cure=float(rec['cure_min'])*float(ee['process_multiplier'])
        unload=float(rec['unload_min'])
        dur=load+cure+unload
        cool=float(rule['cooling_fixed_min'])+float(rule['cooling_per_part_min'])*b['count']
        insp=(float(rule['ndt_minutes_fixed'])+float(rule['ndt_minutes_per_part'])*b['ndt_count']+float(config['coupon_panel_inspection_minutes'])*b['coupon_count']) if b['ndt_count']>0 else 0.0
        cand[(b['id'],aid)]={'dur':dur,'load':load,'cure':cure,'cool':cool,'insp':insp,'layout':layout}

print('batch count',len(batches))
for b in batches:
    print(b['id'],b['recipe_id'],b['program_id'],b['parts'],'window',b['release'],b['latest'],'autos',b['autos'])

batch_map={b['id']:b for b in batches}
def partial_feasible(sol):
    by_auto=defaultdict(list); fix=defaultdict(list); load_ev=defaultdict(list); cure_ev=[]; cool_ev=[]; insp_ev=[]
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]; c=cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]
        s=ent['start']; e=s+c['dur']
        if s<b['release']-1e-9 or s>b['latest']+1e-9 or e>H+1e-9: return False
        for mw in maint[ent['autoclave_id']]:
            if interval_overlap(s,e,*mw): return False
        by_auto[ent['autoclave_id']].append((s,e,b))
        for ft,qty in b['fixture_counts'].items(): fix[ft].append((s,e,qty))
        load_ev[autoclaves[ent['autoclave_id']]['operator_skill']].append((s,s+c['load'],1))
        cure_ev.append((s+c['load'],s+c['load']+c['cure'],1))
        cool_ev.append((e,e+c['cool'],1))
        if c['insp']>0: insp_ev.append((e+c['cool'],e+c['cool']+c['insp'],1))
    for aid,lst in by_auto.items():
        lst.sort()
        for i in range(1,len(lst)):
            ps,pe,pb=lst[i-1]; s,e,b=lst[i]
            gap=setup[(recipes[pb['recipe_id']]['resin_family'], recipes[b['recipe_id']]['resin_family'])]
            if s < pe + gap - 1e-9: return False
    def check(events, caps):
        pts=sorted({0.0,H} | {x for s,e,_ in events for x in (s,e)} | {x for s,e,_ in caps for x in (s,e)})
        for l,r in zip(pts,pts[1:]):
            if r-l<=1e-9: continue
            m=(l+r)/2
            usage=sum(v for s,e,v in events if s<=m<e)
            cap=cap_at(caps,m)
            if cap is not None and usage>cap+1e-9: return False
        return True
    if not check(cure_ev, energy): return False
    if not check(cool_ev, cooling): return False
    if not check(insp_ev, inspect): return False
    for skill,ev in load_ev.items():
        if not check(ev, ops[skill]): return False
    for ft,ev in fix.items():
        if not check(ev, [(0.0,H,fixtures[ft][0])]): return False
    return True

def full_validate(sol):
    if len(sol)"'!=len(batches): return False,'"'missing'
    if not partial_feasible(sol): return False,'partial'
    prog=defaultdict(int)
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]
        end=ent['start']+cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]['dur']
        if end <= next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==b['program_id'])+1e-9:
            prog[b['program_id']]+=len(b['parts'])
    for rr in commit:
        total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id'])
        req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total-1e-9)
        if prog[rr['program_id']]<req: return False,('commit',rr['program_id'],prog[rr['program_id']],req)
    return True,'ok'

# search
visited=0; best=None

def candidate_choices(b, sol):
    out=[]
    for aid in b['autos']:
        c=cand[(b['id'],aid)]
        lb=math.ceil(b['release']); ub=math.floor(b['latest'])
        found=0; t=lb
        while t<=ub and found<6:
            ent={'id':b['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':float(t)}
            if partial_feasible(sol+[ent]):
                out.append((t+c['dur'], len(b['autos']), t, aid, float(t)))
                found+=1
                t += 20
            else:
                t += 1
    out.sort()
    return [(aid,t) for _,_,__,aid,t in out]

def optimistic_commit(sol, unscheduled):
    prog=defaultdict(int)
    remain=defaultdict(int)
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]
        end=ent['start']+cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]['dur']
        if end <= next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==b['program_id'])+1e-9:
            prog[b['program_id']]+=len(b['parts'])
    for b in unscheduled:
        if b['release'] + min(cand[(b['id'],aid)]['dur'] for aid in b['autos']) <= next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==b['program_id'])+1e-9:
            remain[b['program_id']]+=len(b['parts'])
    for rr in commit:
        total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id'])
        req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total-1e-9)
        if prog[rr['program_id']] + remain[rr['program_id']] < req:
            return False
    return True

def search(sol, unscheduled):
    global visited,best
    visited += 1
    if time.time()-start_all > 110:
        return False
    if not unscheduled:
        ok,msg=full_validate(sol)
        print('leaf',ok,msg,'visited',visited)
        if ok:
            best=list(sol)
            return True
        return False
    if not optimistic_commit(sol, unscheduled):
        return False
    domains=[]
    for b in unscheduled:
        ch=candidate_choices(b, sol)
        if not ch: return False
        domains.append((len(ch), len(b['autos']), b['latest']-b['release'], b['latest'], b, ch))
    domains.sort(key=lambda x:(x[0],x[1],x[2],x[3]))
    _,_,_,_,b,ch=domains[0]
    rest=[u for u in unscheduled if u['id']"'!=b['"'id']]
    for aid,t in ch:
        sol.append({'id':b['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':t})
        if search(sol, rest): return True
        sol.pop()
    return False

ok=search([], batches)
print('ok',ok,'visited',visited,'elapsed',time.time()-start_all)
print('best',best)
if best:
    for ent in sorted(best,key=lambda x:x['start']):
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]
        c=cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]
        print(ent['id'],b['recipe_id'],b['program_id'],ent['autoclave_id'],ent['start'],ent['start']+c['dur'])

def batch_ok(part_ids):

    rid=grp[0]['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]

    return feas,(rel,latest)

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import csv, json, math, itertools, time
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autoclaves={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
recipes={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipes.csv'))}
elig=defaultdict(dict)
for e in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')): elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}
vm=defaultdict(dict)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/vacuum_manifold_limits.csv')): vm[r['autoclave_id']][r['side']]=int(r['max_vacuum_ports'])
fixtures={r['fixture_type']:(int(r['capacity']),float(r['setup_buffer_min'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/fixtures.csv'))}
setup={(r['from_resin_family'],r['to_resin_family']):float(r['setup_min']) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/setup_times.csv'))}
maint=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/maintenance_windows.csv')): maint[r['autoclave_id']].append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min'])))
ops=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/operator_calendar.csv')): ops[r['skill']].append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])))
energy=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['max_active_autoclaves'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/energy_tariffs.csv'))]
cooling=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/cooling_lane_capacity.csv'))]
inspect=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/post_cure_inspection_capacity.csv'))]
commit=[r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/program_commitments.csv'))]
config=json.load(open('/app/data/config.json'))
H=float(config['planning_horizon_min'])
start_all=time.time()

def cap_at(intervals,t):
    for s,e,c in intervals:
        if s<=t<e: return c
    return None

def interval_overlap(a,b,c,d):
    return max(a,c)<min(b,d)-1e-9

def dims(p,rot):
    w,h=float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])
    return (h,w) if rot else (w,h)

def feasible_pref(side,Wfull,Hh,x,y,w,h,pref):
    gx=x if side=='left' else Wfull/2+x
    if pref=='center': return gx>1e-6 and y>1e-6 and gx+w<Wfull-1e-6 and y+h<Hh-1e-6
    if pref=='edge': return gx<=1e-6 or y<=1e-6 or gx+w>=Wfull-1e-6 or y+h>=Hh-1e-6
    return True

def backtrack_pack(items, side, Wside, Wfull, Hh):
    items=sorted(items,key=lambda it:(it['pref']=='edge',it['pref']"'!='"'center',-it['h'],-it['w'],it['id']),reverse=True)
    placed=[]
    def rec(k):
        if k==len(items): return True
        it=items[k]
        xs={0.0,max(0.0,Wside-it['w'])}; ys={0.0,max(0.0,Hh-it['h'])}
        for _,px,py,pw,ph,_ in placed:
            xs.add(px+pw); ys.add(py+ph)
        for y in sorted(ys):
            for x in sorted(xs):
                if x+it['w']>Wside+1e-9 or y+it['h']>Hh+1e-9: continue
                if any(max(x,px)<min(x+it['w'],px+pw)-1e-9 and max(y,py)<min(y+it['h'],py+ph)-1e-9 for _,px,py,pw,ph,_ in placed):
                    continue
                if not feasible_pref(side,Wfull,Hh,x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['pref']): continue
                placed.append((it['id'],x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['rot']))
                if rec(k+1): return True
                placed.pop()
        return False
    if rec(0): return {pid:(x,y,w,h,rot) for pid,x,y,w,h,rot in placed}
    return None

mip_cache={}
def mip_layout(grp, aid):
    key=(tuple(sorted(p['part_id'] for p in grp)),aid)
    if key in mip_cache: return mip_cache[key]
    A=autoclaves[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); Hh=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    m=Model(); m.setParam('display/verblevel',0); m.setParam('limits/time',4)
    x={}; y={}; rot={}; left={}; right={}; widths={}; heights={}
    for p in grp:
        pid=p['part_id']; x[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0,ub=W); y[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0,ub=Hh)
        rot[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B',lb=0,ub=1 if int(p['rotate_ok']) else 0)
        left[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B'); right[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B'); m.addCons(left[pid]+right[pid]==1)
        w0=float(p['width_cm']); h0=float(p['height_cm'])
        widths[pid]=w0+(h0-w0)*rot[pid]; heights[pid]=h0+(w0-h0)*rot[pid]
        m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid]<=W); m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid]<=Hh)
        m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid]<=half + W*(1-left[pid])); m.addCons(x[pid]>=half-W*(1-right[pid]))
        pref=p['preferred_zone']
        if pref=='center':
            m.addCons(x[pid]>=1e-3); m.addCons(y[pid]>=1e-3); m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid]<=W-1e-3); m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid]<=Hh-1e-3)
        elif pref=='edge':
            eL=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eR=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eB=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eT=m.addVar(vtype='B')
            m.addCons(eL+eR+eB+eT>=1)
            m.addCons(x[pid]<=W*(1-eL)); m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid]>=W-W*(1-eR)); m.addCons(y[pid]<=Hh*(1-eB)); m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid]>=Hh-Hh*(1-eT))
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*left[p['part_id']] for p in grp)<=vm[aid]['left'])
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*right[p['part_id']] for p in grp)<=vm[aid]['right'])
    ids=[p['part_id'] for p in grp]
    for i in range(len(ids)):
        for j in range(i+1,len(ids)):
            a,b=ids[i],ids[j]
            L=m.addVar(vtype='B'); R=m.addVar(vtype='B'); B=m.addVar(vtype='B'); T=m.addVar(vtype='B')
            m.addCons(L+R+B+T>=1)
            m.addCons(x[a]+widths[a]<=x[b]+W*(1-L)); m.addCons(x[b]+widths[b]<=x[a]+W*(1-R)); m.addCons(y[a]+heights[a]<=y[b]+Hh*(1-B)); m.addCons(y[b]+heights[b]<=y[a]+Hh*(1-T))
    m.setObjective(quicksum(x[p]+y[p] for p in ids),'minimize'); m.optimize()
    if m.getNSols()==0: mip_cache[key]=None; return None
    s=m.getBestSol(); out=[{'part_id':pid,'x_cm':m.getSolVal(s,x[pid]),'y_cm':m.getSolVal(s,y[pid]),'rotated':bool(round(m.getSolVal(s,rot[pid])))} for pid in ids]
    mip_cache[key]=out; return out

def layout_for(grp, aid):
    A=autoclaves[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); Hh=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    rid=grp[0]['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]
    if sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in grp)>float(rule['max_area_fraction'])*W*Hh+1e-9: return None
    if len(grp)>int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']) or sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)>int(rule['max_coupon_panels']) or len({p['program_id'] for p in grp})>int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']): return None
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in grp)>int(A['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in grp)>int(A['thermocouple_ports']): return None
    e=elig[rid].get(aid)
    if not e: return None
    for p in grp:
        if float(p['thickness_mm'])>float(e['max_thickness_mm'])+1e-9 or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])>int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']): return None
    rot_opts=[[0,1] if int(p['rotate_ok']) else [0] for p in grp]
    for rots in itertools.product(*rot_opts):
        items=[]; bad=False
        for p,r in zip(grp,rots):
            w,h=dims(p,bool(r))
            if w>half+1e-9 or h>Hh+1e-9: bad=True; break
            items.append({'id':p['part_id'],'w':w,'h':h,'pref':p['preferred_zone'],'rot':bool(r),'vac':int(p['vacuum_ports'])})
        if bad: continue
        for sides in itertools.product(['left','right'], repeat=len(items)):
            lv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left'); rv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right')
            if lv>vm[aid]['left'] or rv>vm[aid]['right']: continue
            lp=backtrack_pack([it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left'],'left',half,W,Hh)
            if lp is None: continue
            rp=backtrack_pack([it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right'],'right',half,W,Hh)
            if rp is None: continue
            out=[]
            for it,s in zip(items,sides):
                x,y,w,h,rot=(lp if s=='left' else rp)[it['id']]
                out.append({'part_id':it['id'],'x_cm':x if s=='left' else half+x,'y_cm':y,'rotated':rot})
            return out
    return mip_layout(grp,aid)

batch_defs=[
('B001',['P018','P003','P048','P054','P033']),
('B002',['P001','P031','P016','P046']),
('B003',['P029','P014','P055','P044','P064','P065']),

('B014',['P019','P004','P034','P049','P059']),

batches=[]
cand={}
for bid,pids in batch_defs:
    grp=[parts[p] for p in pids]
    b={'id':bid,'recipe_id':grp[0]['recipe_id'],'program_id':grp[0]['program_id'],'parts':pids}
    b['programs']=sorted({p['program_id'] for p in grp})
    b['release']=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in grp)
    b['latest']=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in grp)
    b['due']=min(float(p['due_min']) for p in grp)
    b['fixture_counts']=Counter(p['fixture_type'] for p in grp)
    b['count']=len(grp); b['ndt_count']=sum(int(p['ndt_required']) for p in grp); b['coupon_count']=sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)
    b['autos']=[]
    for aid in autoclaves:
        lay=layout_for(grp,aid)
        if lay is None: continue
        b['autos'].append(aid)
        rec=recipes[b['recipe_id']]; ee=elig[b['recipe_id']][aid]; rule=rules[b['recipe_id']]
        load=float(rec['load_fixed_min'])+float(rec['load_per_part_min'])*b['count']; cure=float(rec['cure_min'])*float(ee['process_multiplier']); unload=float(rec['unload_min'])
        dur=load+cure+unload; cool=float(rule['cooling_fixed_min'])+float(rule['cooling_per_part_min'])*b['count']; insp=(float(rule['ndt_minutes_fixed'])+float(rule['ndt_minutes_per_part'])*b['ndt_count']+float(config['coupon_panel_inspection_minutes'])*b['coupon_count']) if b['ndt_count']>0 else 0.0
        cand[(bid,aid)]={'dur':dur,'load':load,'cure':cure,'cool':cool,'insp':insp,'layout':lay}
    batches.append(b)
print('batches')
for b in batches: print(b['id'],b['recipe_id'],b['programs'],'window',b['release'],b['latest'],'autos',b['autos'])

batch_map={b['id']:b for b in batches}
def partial_feasible(sol):
    by_auto=defaultdict(list); fix=defaultdict(list); load_ev=defaultdict(list); cure_ev=[]; cool_ev=[]; insp_ev=[]
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]; c=cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]; s=ent['start']; e=s+c['dur']
        if s<b['release']-1e-9 or s>b['latest']+1e-9 or e>H+1e-9: return False
        for mw in maint[ent['autoclave_id']]:
            if interval_overlap(s,e,*mw): return False
        by_auto[ent['autoclave_id']].append((s,e,b))
        for ft,qty in b['fixture_counts'].items(): fix[ft].append((s,e,qty))
        load_ev[autoclaves[ent['autoclave_id']]['operator_skill']].append((s,s+c['load'],1)); cure_ev.append((s+c['load'],s+c['load']+c['cure'],1)); cool_ev.append((e,e+c['cool'],1))
        if c['insp']>0: insp_ev.append((e+c['cool'],e+c['cool']+c['insp'],1))
    for aid,lst in by_auto.items():
        lst.sort()
        for i in range(1,len(lst)):
            ps,pe,pb=lst[i-1]; s,e,b=lst[i]
            gap=setup[(recipes[pb['recipe_id']]['resin_family'], recipes[b['recipe_id']]['resin_family'])]
            if s < pe + gap - 1e-9: return False
    def check(events,caps):
        pts=sorted({0.0,H} | {x for s,e,_ in events for x in (s,e)} | {x for s,e,_ in caps for x in (s,e)})
        for l,r in zip(pts,pts[1:]):
            if r-l<=1e-9: continue
            m=(l+r)/2; use=sum(v for s,e,v in events if s<=m<e); cap=cap_at(caps,m)
            if cap is not None and use>cap+1e-9: return False
        return True
    if not check(cure_ev,energy): return False
    if not check(cool_ev,cooling): return False
    if not check(insp_ev,inspect): return False
    for sk,ev in load_ev.items():
        if not check(ev,ops[sk]): return False
    for ft,ev in fix.items():
        if not check(ev,[(0.0,H,fixtures[ft][0])]): return False
    return True

def full_validate(sol):
    if len(sol)"'!=len(batches) or not partial_feasible(sol): return False
    prog=defaultdict(int)
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['"'id']]; end=ent['start']+cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]['dur']
        for prog_id in b['programs']:
            due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
            if end<=due+1e-9:
                prog[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in b['parts'] if parts[p]['program_id']==prog_id)
    for rr in commit:
        total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id'])
        req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total-1e-9)
        if prog[rr['program_id']]<req: return False
    return True

visited=0; best=None

def candidate_choices(b,sol):
    out=[]
    for aid in b['autos']:
        c=cand[(b['id'],aid)]; lb=math.ceil(b['release']); ub=math.floor(b['latest']); found=0; t=lb
        while t<=ub and found<10:
            ent={'id':b['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':float(t)}
            if partial_feasible(sol+[ent]):
                out.append((t+c['dur'], len(b['autos']), t, aid, float(t))); found+=1; t+=20
            else:
                t+=1
    out.sort(); return [(aid,t) for _,_,__,aid,t in out]

def optimistic(sol,uns):
    prog=defaultdict(int); rem=defaultdict(int)
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]; end=ent['start']+cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]['dur']
        for prog_id in b['programs']:
            due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
            if end<=due+1e-9: prog[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in b['parts'] if parts[p]['program_id']==prog_id)
    for b in uns:
        minend=b['release']+min(cand[(b['id'],aid)]['dur'] for aid in b['autos'])
        for prog_id in b['programs']:
            due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
            if minend<=due+1e-9: rem[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in b['parts'] if parts[p]['program_id']==prog_id)
    for rr in commit:
        total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id']); req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total-1e-9)
        if prog[rr['program_id']]+rem[rr['program_id']]<req: return False
    return True

def search(sol,uns):
    global visited,best
    visited+=1
    if time.time()-start_all>120: return False
    if not uns:
        ok=full_validate(sol); print('leaf',ok,'visited',visited)
        if ok: best=list(sol); return True
        return False
    if not optimistic(sol,uns): return False
    domains=[]
    for b in uns:
        ch=candidate_choices(b,sol)
        if not ch: return False
        domains.append((len(ch),len(b['autos']),b['latest']-b['release'],b['latest'],b,ch))
    domains.sort(key=lambda x:(x[0],x[1],x[2],x[3]))
    _,_,_,_,b,ch=domains[0]; rest=[u for u in uns if u['id']"'!=b['"'id']]
    for aid,t in ch:
        sol.append({'id':b['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':t})
        if search(sol,rest): return True
        sol.pop()
    return False

ok=search([],batches)
print('ok',ok,'visited',visited,'elapsed',time.time()-start_all)
print(best)
if best:
    for ent in sorted(best,key=lambda x:x['start']):
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]; c=cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]
        print(ent['id'],b['recipe_id'],b['programs'],ent['autoclave_id'],ent['start'],ent['start']+c['dur'])

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import csv, json, math, itertools, time
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum
parts={p['part_id']:p for p in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/parts.csv'))}
autoclaves={a['autoclave_id']:a for a in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclaves.csv'))}
recipes={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipes.csv'))}
elig=defaultdict(dict)
for e in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/autoclave_recipe_eligibility.csv')): elig[e['recipe_id']][e['autoclave_id']]=e
rules={r['recipe_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/recipe_batch_rules.csv'))}
vm=defaultdict(dict)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/vacuum_manifold_limits.csv')): vm[r['autoclave_id']][r['side']]=int(r['max_vacuum_ports'])
fixtures={r['fixture_type']:(int(r['capacity']),float(r['setup_buffer_min'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/fixtures.csv'))}
setup={(r['from_resin_family'],r['to_resin_family']):float(r['setup_min']) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/setup_times.csv'))}
maint=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/maintenance_windows.csv')): maint[r['autoclave_id']].append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min'])))
ops=defaultdict(list)
for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/operator_calendar.csv')): ops[r['skill']].append((float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])))
energy=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['max_active_autoclaves'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/energy_tariffs.csv'))]
cooling=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/cooling_lane_capacity.csv'))]
inspect=[(float(r['start_min']),float(r['end_min']),int(r['capacity'])) for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/post_cure_inspection_capacity.csv'))]
commit=[r for r in csv.DictReader(open('/app/data/program_commitments.csv'))]
config=json.load(open('/app/data/config.json'))
H=float(config['planning_horizon_min'])
start_all=time.time()

def cap_at(intervals,t):
    for s,e,c in intervals:
        if s<=t<e: return c
    return None

def interval_overlap(a,b,c,d):
    return max(a,c)<min(b,d)-1e-9

def dims(p,rot):
    w,h=float(p['width_cm']),float(p['height_cm'])
    return (h,w) if rot else (w,h)

def feasible_pref(side,Wfull,Hh,x,y,w,h,pref):
    gx=x if side=='left' else Wfull/2+x
    if pref=='center': return gx>1e-6 and y>1e-6 and gx+w<Wfull-1e-6 and y+h<Hh-1e-6
    if pref=='edge': return gx<=1e-6 or y<=1e-6 or gx+w>=Wfull-1e-6 or y+h>=Hh-1e-6
    return True

def backtrack_pack(items, side, Wside, Wfull, Hh):
    items=sorted(items,key=lambda it:(it['pref']=='edge',it['pref']"'!='"'center',-it['h'],-it['w'],it['id']),reverse=True)
    placed=[]
    def rec(k):
        if k==len(items): return True
        it=items[k]
        xs={0.0,max(0.0,Wside-it['w'])}; ys={0.0,max(0.0,Hh-it['h'])}
        for _,px,py,pw,ph,_ in placed:
            xs.add(px+pw); ys.add(py+ph)
        for y in sorted(ys):
            for x in sorted(xs):
                if x+it['w']>Wside+1e-9 or y+it['h']>Hh+1e-9: continue
                if any(max(x,px)<min(x+it['w'],px+pw)-1e-9 and max(y,py)<min(y+it['h'],py+ph)-1e-9 for _,px,py,pw,ph,_ in placed): continue
                if not feasible_pref(side,Wfull,Hh,x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['pref']): continue
                placed.append((it['id'],x,y,it['w'],it['h'],it['rot']))
                if rec(k+1): return True
                placed.pop()
        return False
    if rec(0): return {pid:(x,y,w,h,rot) for pid,x,y,w,h,rot in placed}
    return None

mip_cache={}
def mip_layout(grp, aid):
    key=(tuple(sorted(p['part_id'] for p in grp)),aid)
    if key in mip_cache: return mip_cache[key]
    A=autoclaves[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); Hh=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    m=Model(); m.setParam('display/verblevel',0); m.setParam('limits/time',4)
    x={}; y={}; rot={}; left={}; right={}; widths={}; heights={}
    for p in grp:
        pid=p['part_id']; x[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0,ub=W); y[pid]=m.addVar(lb=0,ub=Hh)
        rot[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B',lb=0,ub=1 if int(p['rotate_ok']) else 0)
        left[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B'); right[pid]=m.addVar(vtype='B'); m.addCons(left[pid]+right[pid]==1)
        w0=float(p['width_cm']); h0=float(p['height_cm'])
        widths[pid]=w0+(h0-w0)*rot[pid]; heights[pid]=h0+(w0-h0)*rot[pid]
        m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid]<=W); m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid]<=Hh)
        m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid]<=half+W*(1-left[pid])); m.addCons(x[pid]>=half-W*(1-right[pid]))
        pref=p['preferred_zone']
        if pref=='center':
            m.addCons(x[pid]>=1e-3); m.addCons(y[pid]>=1e-3); m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid]<=W-1e-3); m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid]<=Hh-1e-3)
        elif pref=='edge':
            eL=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eR=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eB=m.addVar(vtype='B'); eT=m.addVar(vtype='B')
            m.addCons(eL+eR+eB+eT>=1)
            m.addCons(x[pid]<=W*(1-eL)); m.addCons(x[pid]+widths[pid]>=W-W*(1-eR)); m.addCons(y[pid]<=Hh*(1-eB)); m.addCons(y[pid]+heights[pid]>=Hh-Hh*(1-eT))
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*left[p['part_id']] for p in grp)<=vm[aid]['left'])
    m.addCons(quicksum(int(p['vacuum_ports'])*right[p['part_id']] for p in grp)<=vm[aid]['right'])
    ids=[p['part_id'] for p in grp]
    for i in range(len(ids)):
        for j in range(i+1,len(ids)):
            a,b=ids[i],ids[j]
            L=m.addVar(vtype='B'); R=m.addVar(vtype='B'); B=m.addVar(vtype='B'); T=m.addVar(vtype='B')
            m.addCons(L+R+B+T>=1)
            m.addCons(x[a]+widths[a]<=x[b]+W*(1-L)); m.addCons(x[b]+widths[b]<=x[a]+W*(1-R)); m.addCons(y[a]+heights[a]<=y[b]+Hh*(1-B)); m.addCons(y[b]+heights[b]<=y[a]+Hh*(1-T))
    m.setObjective(quicksum(x[p]+y[p] for p in ids),'minimize'); m.optimize()
    if m.getNSols()==0: mip_cache[key]=None; return None
    s=m.getBestSol(); out=[{'part_id':pid,'x_cm':m.getSolVal(s,x[pid]),'y_cm':m.getSolVal(s,y[pid]),'rotated':bool(round(m.getSolVal(s,rot[pid])))} for pid in ids]
    mip_cache[key]=out; return out

def layout_for(grp, aid):
    A=autoclaves[aid]; W=float(A['width_cm']); Hh=float(A['height_cm']); half=W/2
    rid=grp[0]['recipe_id']; rule=rules[rid]
    if sum(float(p['width_cm'])*float(p['height_cm']) for p in grp)>float(rule['max_area_fraction'])*W*Hh+1e-9: return None
    if len(grp)>int(rule['max_parts_per_batch']) or sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp)>int(rule['max_coupon_panels']) or len({p['program_id'] for p in grp})>int(rule['max_programs_per_batch']): return None
    if sum(int(p['vacuum_ports']) for p in grp)>int(A['vacuum_ports']) or sum(int(p['thermocouples']) for p in grp)>int(A['thermocouple_ports']): return None
    e=elig[rid].get(aid)
    if not e: return None
    for p in grp:
        if float(p['thickness_mm'])>float(e['max_thickness_mm'])+1e-9 or int(p['requires_ultra_uniform'])>int(e['supports_ultra_uniform']): return None
    rot_opts=[[0,1] if int(p['rotate_ok']) else [0] for p in grp]
    for rots in itertools.product(*rot_opts):
        items=[]; bad=False
        for p,r in zip(grp,rots):
            w,h=dims(p,bool(r))
            if w>half+1e-9 or h>Hh+1e-9: bad=True; break
            items.append({'id':p['part_id'],'w':w,'h':h,'pref':p['preferred_zone'],'rot':bool(r),'vac':int(p['vacuum_ports'])})
        if bad: continue
        for sides in itertools.product(['left','right'], repeat=len(items)):
            lv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left'); rv=sum(it['vac'] for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right')
            if lv>vm[aid]['left'] or rv>vm[aid]['right']: continue
            lp=backtrack_pack([it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='left'],'left',half,W,Hh)
            if lp is None: continue
            rp=backtrack_pack([it for it,s in zip(items,sides) if s=='right'],'right',half,W,Hh)
            if rp is None: continue
            out=[]
            for it,s in zip(items,sides):
                x,y,w,h,rot=(lp if s=='left' else rp)[it['id']]
                out.append({'part_id':it['id'],'x_cm':x if s=='left' else half+x,'y_cm':y,'rotated':rot})
            return out
    return mip_layout(grp,aid)

batches=[]; cand={}
for bid,pids in batch_defs:
    grp=[parts[p] for p in pids]
    b={'id':bid,'recipe_id':grp[0]['recipe_id'],'parts':pids,'programs':sorted({p['program_id'] for p in grp})}
    b['release']=max(float(p['release_min']) for p in grp); b['latest']=min(float(p['release_min'])+float(p['max_out_time_min']) for p in grp); b['due']=min(float(p['due_min']) for p in grp)
    b['fixture_counts']=Counter(p['fixture_type'] for p in grp); b['count']=len(grp); b['ndt_count']=sum(int(p['ndt_required']) for p in grp); b['coupon_count']=sum(int(p['coupon_panels']) for p in grp); b['autos']=[]
    for aid in autoclaves:
        lay=layout_for(grp,aid)
        if lay is None: continue
        b['autos'].append(aid)
        rec=recipes[b['recipe_id']]; ee=elig[b['recipe_id']][aid]; rule=rules[b['recipe_id']]
        load=float(rec['load_fixed_min'])+float(rec['load_per_part_min'])*b['count']; cure=float(rec['cure_min'])*float(ee['process_multiplier']); unload=float(rec['unload_min'])
        dur=load+cure+unload; cool=float(rule['cooling_fixed_min'])+float(rule['cooling_per_part_min'])*b['count']; insp=(float(rule['ndt_minutes_fixed'])+float(rule['ndt_minutes_per_part'])*b['ndt_count']+float(config['coupon_panel_inspection_minutes'])*b['coupon_count']) if b['ndt_count']>0 else 0.0
        cand[(bid,aid)]={'dur':dur,'load':load,'cure':cure,'cool':cool,'insp':insp,'layout':lay}
    batches.append(b)

def partial_feasible(sol):
    by_auto=defaultdict(list); fix=defaultdict(list); load_ev=defaultdict(list); cure_ev=[]; cool_ev=[]; insp_ev=[]
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]; c=cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]; s=ent['start']; e=s+c['dur']
        if s<b['release']-1e-9 or s>b['latest']+1e-9 or e>H+1e-9: return False
        for mw in maint[ent['autoclave_id']]:
            if interval_overlap(s,e,*mw): return False
        by_auto[ent['autoclave_id']].append((s,e,b))
        for ft,qty in b['fixture_counts'].items(): fix[ft].append((s,e,qty))
        load_ev[autoclaves[ent['autoclave_id']]['operator_skill']].append((s,s+c['load'],1)); cure_ev.append((s+c['load'],s+c['load']+c['cure'],1)); cool_ev.append((e,e+c['cool'],1))
        if c['insp']>0: insp_ev.append((e+c['cool'],e+c['cool']+c['insp'],1))
    for aid,lst in by_auto.items():
        lst.sort()
        for i in range(1,len(lst)):
            ps,pe,pb=lst[i-1]; s,e,b=lst[i]
            gap=setup[(recipes[pb['recipe_id']]['resin_family'], recipes[b['recipe_id']]['resin_family'])]
            if s < pe + gap - 1e-9: return False
    def check(events,caps):
        pts=sorted({0.0,H} | {x for s,e,_ in events for x in (s,e)} | {x for s,e,_ in caps for x in (s,e)})
        for l,r in zip(pts,pts[1:]):
            if r-l<=1e-9: continue
            m=(l+r)/2; use=sum(v for s,e,v in events if s<=m<e); cap=cap_at(caps,m)
            if cap is not None and use>cap+1e-9: return False
        return True
    if not check(cure_ev,energy): return False
    if not check(cool_ev,cooling): return False
    if not check(insp_ev,inspect): return False
    for sk,ev in load_ev.items():
        if not check(ev,ops[sk]): return False
    for ft,ev in fix.items():
        if not check(ev,[(0.0,H,fixtures[ft][0])]): return False
    return True

def full_validate(sol):
    if len(sol)"'!=len(batches) or not partial_feasible(sol): return False
    prog=defaultdict(int)
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['"'id']]; end=ent['start']+cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]['dur']
        for prog_id in b['programs']:
            due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
            if end<=due+1e-9: prog[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in b['parts'] if parts[p]['program_id']==prog_id)
    for rr in commit:
        total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id']); req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total-1e-9)
        if prog[rr['program_id']]<req: return False
    return True

visited=0; best=None

def candidate_choices(b,sol):
    out=[]
    for aid in b['autos']:
        c=cand[(b['id'],aid)]; lb=math.ceil(b['release']); ub=math.floor(b['latest']); found=0; t=lb
        while t<=ub and found<15:
            ent={'id':b['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':float(t)}
            if partial_feasible(sol+[ent]):
                out.append((t+c['dur'], len(b['autos']), t, aid, float(t))); found+=1; t+=15
            else:
                t+=1
    out.sort(); return [(aid,t) for _,_,__,aid,t in out]

def optimistic(sol,uns):
    prog=defaultdict(int); rem=defaultdict(int)
    for ent in sol:
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]; end=ent['start']+cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]['dur']
        for prog_id in b['programs']:
            due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
            if end<=due+1e-9: prog[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in b['parts'] if parts[p]['program_id']==prog_id)
    for b in uns:
        minend=b['release']+min(cand[(b['id'],aid)]['dur'] for aid in b['autos'])
        for prog_id in b['programs']:
            due=next(float(r['commitment_due_min']) for r in commit if r['program_id']==prog_id)
            if minend<=due+1e-9: rem[prog_id]+=sum(1 for p in b['parts'] if parts[p]['program_id']==prog_id)
    for rr in commit:
        total=sum(1 for p in parts.values() if p['program_id']==rr['program_id']); req=math.ceil(float(rr['min_on_time_fraction'])*total-1e-9)
        if prog[rr['program_id']]+rem[rr['program_id']]<req: return False
    return True

def search(sol,uns):
    global visited,best
    visited+=1
    if time.time()-start_all>120: return False
    if not uns:
        ok=full_validate(sol); print('leaf',ok,'visited',visited)
        if ok: best=list(sol); return True
        return False
    if not optimistic(sol,uns): return False
    domains=[]
    for b in uns:
        ch=candidate_choices(b,sol)
        if not ch: return False
        domains.append((len(ch),len(b['autos']),b['latest']-b['release'],b['latest'],b,ch))
    domains.sort(key=lambda x:(x[0],x[1],x[2],x[3]))
    _,_,_,_,b,ch=domains[0]; rest=[u for u in uns if u['id']"'!=b['"'id']]
    for aid,t in ch:
        sol.append({'id':b['id'],'autoclave_id':aid,'start':t})
        if search(sol,rest): return True
        sol.pop()
    return False

ok=search([],batches)
print('ok',ok,'visited',visited,'elapsed',time.time()-start_all)
print(best)
if best:
    for ent in sorted(best,key=lambda x:x['start']):
        b=batch_map[ent['id']]; c=cand[(b['id'],ent['autoclave_id'])]
        print(ent['id'],b['recipe_id'],b['programs'],ent['autoclave_id'],ent['start'],ent['start']+c['dur'])
